产业链研报 · 存储 · AI 数据中心Industry research · Memory and storage · AI data centers

记忆之墙：从 SRAM 到磁带，
AI 让每一层存储都成了瓶颈The Memory Wall: from SRAM to tape,
AI has turned every tier into a bottleneck

本报告把 AI 数据中心的存储拆成九层（片上 SRAM、HBM、CPU 侧 DRAM、CXL 内存、存储级内存、NAND 企业级 SSD、高带宽闪存、HDD、磁带），逐层讲清它负责什么、为什么难造、用量多少、供需如何；再把大模型训练、推理与 AI Agent 的完整流程逐步拆开，回答每一步卡在哪里、最缺哪种存储、这种存储往哪里演进、有没有绕开的路。最后补上产业经济、被忽略的风险和一份可证伪的跟踪清单。This report splits AI data-center memory into nine tiers (on-chip SRAM, HBM, CPU-side DRAM, CXL memory, storage-class memory, NAND enterprise SSD, high-bandwidth flash, HDD and tape). For each it explains what the tier does, why it is hard to make, how much is used and where supply stands. It then walks through LLM training, inference and AI agents step by step: where each step is bound, which memory is scarcest, where that memory is heading and whether anything routes around it. It closes with industry economics, overlooked risks and a falsifiable tracking list.

十条核心结论Ten core conclusions

  1. 存储已从"配角"变成 AI 的约束条件。Memory has become AI's binding constraint. 过去二十年峰值算力每两年约增长 3.0 倍，DRAM 带宽只有约 1.6 倍S9A。从 H100 到 Rubin，单 GPU 低精度算力约增长 25 倍，HBM 带宽约 6.6 倍、容量约 3.6 倍D。于是 2026 年存储预计占全球半导体营收约 53–54%（2025 年约 28–29%）S325S327A；SK 海力士 2Q26 营业利润率 76%，美光 FQ4 FY26 毛利率 87%S227S162A。Over twenty years peak compute grew ~3.0× every two years while DRAM bandwidth grew ~1.6×S9A. From H100 to Rubin, per-GPU low-precision compute rose ~25×, HBM bandwidth ~6.6× and capacity ~3.6×D. Memory is therefore set to be ~53–54% of 2026 chip revenue, up from ~28–29% in 2025S325S327A; SK hynix earned a 76% operating margin in 2Q26 and Micron an 87% gross margin in FQ4 FY26S227S162A.
  2. 训练和推理卡在不同的层。Training and inference are bound by different tiers. 训练的前向/反向主要卡 HBM 容量（混合精度 Adam 约 16 字节/参数，1T 参数约需 16 TB 模型状态）与 scale-up 互联S2A；十万卡级训练还卡在 checkpoint 的突发写带宽和故障恢复，Llama 3 的 419 次非计划中断中 HBM3 占 17.2%S1A。推理的 decode 卡 HBM 带宽（每生成一个 token 都要把权重和全部 KV 读一遍）；长上下文与多轮对话卡 KV 容量，沿 HBM → CPU 内存 → 本地 NVMe → 机架级闪存（G3.5）→ 共享存储逐层外溢S38S30A。数据湖与产物留存卡近线 HDD。Training forward/backward passes are bound by HBM capacity (mixed-precision Adam needs ~16 bytes per parameter, so 1T parameters need ~16 TB of model state) and by scale-up interconnectS2A; at 100K-GPU scale they are also bound by checkpoint burst writes and failure recovery, and HBM3 caused 17.2% of Llama 3's 419 unplanned interruptionsS1A. Inference decode is bound by HBM bandwidth (every generated token re-reads the weights and the whole KV cache); long contexts and multi-turn chats are bound by KV capacity, which spills from HBM to CPU memory, local NVMe, rack-level flash (G3.5) and shared storageS38S30A. Data lakes and retained artifacts are bound by nearline HDD.
  3. 当下最缺的是 HBM，其次是通用 DRAM，再其次是企业级 SSD 与近线 HDD。Scarcity ranking: HBM first, commodity DRAM second, then enterprise SSD and nearline HDD. JPMorgan 估 HBM 短缺 20%/19%/16%（2026/27/28）S190B，美光称只能满足部分关键客户 50% 至三分之二的需求S164A。HBM 每 bit 约吃掉 2.5–2.9 倍晶圆：2026 年用 22% 的 DRAM 晶圆只产出 9% 的 bit，2027 年是 30% 对 13%S167BD，从而挤压通用 DRAM，服务器 DRAM 2026 年合约价累计约 +270%S211B。企业级 SSD bit 需求 2026 年 +80% 以上S209B；近线 HDD 交期超过 52 周，2027 年产出大多已被长约锁定S272S318BA。JPMorgan sees HBM short by 20%/19%/16% in 2026/27/28S190B, and Micron says it can meet only 50% to two-thirds of some key customers' demandS164A. HBM eats ~2.5–2.9× the wafer per bit: in 2026 it takes 22% of DRAM wafers for 9% of bits, in 2027 30% for 13%S167BD. That squeezes commodity DRAM, and server DRAM contract prices rise ~270% over 2026S211B. Enterprise SSD bit demand grows more than 80% in 2026S209B; nearline HDD lead times exceed 52 weeks and most 2027 output is under long-term agreementsS272S318BA.
  4. 供给的硬约束是洁净室，而不是资本开支。Cleanrooms, not capex dollars, bind supply. 美光 FY26 净资本开支 273.7 亿美元，仅约占营收 20.5%S162AD。有意义的新增产出要到 2H27–2028：美光爱达荷 ID1 2027 年中、ID2 与广岛 2028 年底、纽约 2030；SK 海力士龙仁 Y1 首个洁净室 2027 年 2 月；三星 P5 2028–2030S161S229S212A。所以 2027 年供给弹性最低，2028–2030 年产能集中释放。Micron's FY26 net capex of $27.37bn was only ~20.5% of revenueS162AD. Meaningful new output arrives in 2H27–2028: Micron Idaho ID1 mid-2027, ID2 and Hiroshima late 2028, New York 2030; SK hynix's first Yongin cleanroom in Feb 2027; Samsung P5 in 2028–2030S161S229S212A. Supply is least elastic in 2027, and a capacity wave lands in 2028–2030.
  5. AI Agent 把瓶颈从"算力"推向"记忆"。AI agents push the bottleneck from compute towards memory. Agent 消耗的 token 约为普通聊天的 4 倍，多智能体约 15 倍S85A；Manus 观察到输入:输出约 100:1、每个任务约 50 次工具调用，KV 缓存命中率是它最重要的成本指标（命中的 token 便宜约 10 倍）S84A。这催生了 KV 外溢分层（G2 CPU 内存、G3.5 机架级闪存），并让 CPU:GPU 配比从 1:8 走向 1:4 乃至 1:2S87B，把需求从 HBM 扩散到 LPDDR5X/DDR5 与 QLC 企业级 SSD。Agents use ~4× the tokens of chat, and multi-agent systems ~15×S85A. Manus sees ~100:1 input-to-output and ~50 tool calls per task, and calls KV-cache hit rate its most important cost metric because cached tokens are ~10× cheaperS84A. This has created KV spill tiers (G2 CPU memory, G3.5 rack-level flash) and is moving CPU:GPU ratios from 1:8 towards 1:4 or even 1:2S87B, spreading demand from HBM into LPDDR5X/DDR5 and QLC enterprise SSDs.
  6. 算法在压缩"每 token 存储"，但 Jevons 效应目前更强。Algorithms keep shrinking memory per token, but Jevons is winning so far. DeepSeek 每 token 的全局 KV 从 V1 到 V4.1-Flash 缩小约 437 倍（V4.1 为 890 字节/token，HBM 占用约为 V4-Flash 的 1/4、持久化 KV 约 1/8）S95A；FP4 权重、MLA、稀疏注意力与 KV 量化仍在推进。与此同时，谷歌月度 token 处理量 2024-04 至 2026-05 增长约 330 倍（约 16 倍/年）S90BD，DeepSeek 的默认上下文从 128K 走到 1MS97A。压缩最直接威胁的是"按每 token KV 线性外推"的闪存需求预测（例如每 GPU 16 TB），而不是 HBM 的总需求。DeepSeek cut global KV per token ~437× from V1 to V4.1-Flash (890 bytes per token; about 1/4 of V4-Flash's HBM footprint and 1/8 of its persistent KV)S95A; FP4 weights, MLA, sparse attention and KV quantization keep advancing. Meanwhile Google's monthly tokens grew ~330× from Apr 2024 to May 2026 (~16× a year)S90BD, and DeepSeek's default context went from 128K to 1MS97A. Compression most directly threatens flash forecasts that extrapolate KV per token linearly (such as 16 TB per GPU), not total HBM demand.
  7. 多数绕行路径是在层间"搬需求"，而不是消灭需求。Most workarounds move demand between tiers rather than remove it. SRAM 推理（NVIDIA Groq 3 LPX）只接管 FFN/MoE 的权重流式读取，注意力与 KV 仍留在 GPU 的 HBM 里；一整个 LPX 机架 128 GB SRAM，还不到一颗 Rubin 的 288 GB HBM 的一半S31AD。CXL 与光互连内存池把 bit 留在 DRAM；HBF 把容量搬到 NAND；PIM 给 DRAM 增加价值；3D DRAM 与 4F² 增加的是供给。SRAM inference (NVIDIA Groq 3 LPX) takes over only the FFN/MoE weight streaming; attention and the KV cache stay in GPU HBM, and a whole LPX rack's 128 GB of SRAM is less than half of one Rubin's 288 GB of HBMS31AD. CXL and optical memory pools keep the bits in DRAM; HBF moves capacity to NAND; processing-in-memory adds value to DRAM; 3D DRAM and 4F² add supply.
  8. 价值在向逻辑代工、设备与接口芯片扩散。Value is spreading to foundry logic, equipment and interface chips. HBM 基底裸片转向台积电 N12/N3P，成本约为 SK 海力士自研核心裸片的 3–4 倍；三星 4nm 基底裸片涨价 40–50%S172S171B。TC 键合机（韩美半导体 2Q26 营业利润率 51.9%）、存储测试（爱德万份额超过 50%）、RCD/MRCD、CXL 控制器与 SSD 主控都在分享增量S367S180S240BA。长约与预付款改变了周期形态：既有价格下限，也有上限S161S213AB。HBM base dies are moving to TSMC N12/N3P at ~3–4× the cost of SK hynix's own core dies, and Samsung raised its 4nm base-die prices 40–50%S172S171B. TC bonders (Hanmi's 2Q26 operating margin: 51.9%), memory test (Advantest holds over 50%), RCD/MRCD chips, CXL controllers and SSD controllers all share in the growthS367S180S240BA. Long-term agreements with prepayments change the shape of the cycle: they set price floors and also ceilingsS161S213AB.
  9. 最容易被忽略的风险。The most overlooked risks. 涨价斜率快速放缓（DRAM 合约价环比从 1Q26 的 +93–98% 降到 4Q26 的 +10–15%）S206S209B；买家开始"设计掉"存储（英伟达希望内存不超过 BOM 的约 20%、SOCAMM 容量减半、Rubin Ultra 考虑 8 层 HBM）S344S214S215B；消费电子需求被摧毁（IDC：2026 年手机 −16.7%、PC −11.3%）S264S265B；长鑫与长江存储 4Q28 前产能约翻倍S338B；过去 12 个月 32% 的云厂商资本开支靠举债S331B；2026 年计划投运的数据中心容量有 30–50% 可能推迟S148B。Price increases are decelerating fast (DRAM contract prices from +93–98% QoQ in 1Q26 to +10–15% in 4Q26)S206S209B; buyers are designing memory out (NVIDIA wants memory at no more than ~20% of BOM, halved SOCAMM, and is weighing 8-high HBM for Rubin Ultra)S344S214S215B; consumer demand is being destroyed (IDC: 2026 phones −16.7%, PCs −11.3%)S264S265B; CXMT and YMTC roughly double capacity by 4Q28S338B; debt funded 32% of hyperscaler capex over the last twelve monthsS331B; and 30–50% of data-center capacity slated for 2026 may slipS148B.
  10. 一个判断框架。A framework. 看存储周期要同时看"量"和"价"：量由 bit 需求驱动，Jevons 效应目前占上风；价受长约上限、买家替代和 2028 年后的产能浪潮约束。2027 年大概率是供给最紧的一年（SK 海力士 CEO："可能是行业史上供给最差的一年"）S192B，但利润增速可能先于价格见顶。第 19 节给出带日期的可证伪跟踪清单。Read the cycle through volume and price at once. Volume follows bit demand, where Jevons is ahead for now; price is capped by LTA ceilings, buyer substitution and the post-2028 capacity wave. 2027 is likely the tightest supply year (SK hynix's CEO: "could be the worst period in the industry's history from a supply perspective")S192B, yet profit growth may peak before prices do. Section 19 lists dated, falsifiable signals.

01

存储层级总览：为什么 AI 需要一座"金字塔"The hierarchy at a glance: why AI needs a pyramid

没有任何一种存储能同时做到快、大、便宜、断电不丢。物理决定了取舍：SRAM 用 6 个晶体管存 1 个 bit，快但占面积；DRAM 用 1 个晶体管加 1 个电容，密度高但要每 64 毫秒左右刷新一次；NAND 把电荷锁在三维堆叠的存储单元里，断电不丢、便宜，但只能按页读、按块擦，写入寿命有限；HDD 用磁性颗粒记录，单位成本最低，但要靠机械寻道；磁带更便宜，访问以秒到分钟计。所以系统设计永远是一座金字塔：越往上越快越贵越小，越往下越慢越便宜越大。No single memory is fast, large, cheap and persistent at once. Physics forces the trade-off. SRAM stores one bit in six transistors: fast but area-hungry. DRAM uses one transistor and one capacitor: dense, but it must be refreshed roughly every 64 ms. NAND traps charge in 3D-stacked cells: persistent and cheap, but it reads by page, erases by block and wears out. HDDs record on magnetic grains: the lowest cost per bit, but with mechanical seeks. Tape is cheaper still, with access measured in seconds to minutes. So every system is a pyramid: faster, costlier and smaller at the top; slower, cheaper and larger at the bottom.

AI 改变的不是金字塔的形状，而是每一层的"压力"。大模型的计算量随参数和 token 线性增长，但每生成一个 token 都要把权重和上下文（KV 缓存）从存储里搬一遍；算力增长远快于带宽，于是"搬数据"成了主要成本。下表把九层存储放在同一把对数尺子上比较。AI did not change the pyramid's shape; it changed the pressure on each layer. LLM compute scales with parameters and tokens, but every generated token moves the weights and the context (the KV cache) out of memory again. Compute has grown much faster than bandwidth, so moving data has become the main cost. The table puts all nine tiers on the same logarithmic ruler.

九层存储的同一把尺子：延迟、带宽密度、单价与 2026 年供需Nine tiers on one ruler: latency, bandwidth density, price and 2026 supply
条形为对数刻度。带宽密度 = 设备带宽 ÷ 设备容量（GB/s 每 GB），它解释了为什么每一层都不可替代：SRAM 每 GB 的带宽约为 HBM 的 4,300 倍，HBM 又约为 CPU 内存的 100 倍。Bars are logarithmic. Bandwidth density = device bandwidth ÷ device capacity (GB/s per GB). It explains why no tier can replace another: SRAM has ~4,300× HBM's bandwidth per GB, and HBM ~100× CPU memory's.

bit 与美元：DRAM 只占 1% 的 bit，却拿走 2/3 的钱Bits versus dollars: DRAM is ~1% of bits but ~2/3 of the money

把 2026 年各层的出货容量（EB）和营收放在一起看，会发现金字塔顶端的"快存储"以极小的 bit 份额拿走了绝大部分营收。这也是为什么 HBM 与 DRAM 的任何供需变化都会剧烈影响整个半导体行业的利润分配。Put each tier's 2026 shipped capacity (EB) next to its revenue and the top of the pyramid takes most of the money on a tiny share of bits. That is why any shift in HBM or DRAM supply moves profit across the whole chip industry.

HDD's implied ~$15/TB is near hyperscale contract pricing, well below street price.D

四种物理，四种脾气Four physics, four temperaments

SRAM六个晶体管锁存一个 bitSix transistors latch one bit

和逻辑电路用同一种工艺做在芯片上，无需刷新，访问只要几纳秒。代价是面积：TSMC N5 与 N3E 的高密度 SRAM 单元都是 0.021 µm²，几乎停止微缩，N2 也只到约 38 Mb/mm²S198S199B。所以 SRAM 只能做"小而快"的缓存，按裸硅折算每 GB 约 65–110 美元D。Built on the chip in the same process as logic, needs no refresh and answers in nanoseconds. The cost is area: TSMC's high-density SRAM cell is 0.021 µm² on both N5 and N3E, so scaling has nearly stopped, and N2 reaches only ~38 Mb/mm²S198S199B. SRAM can therefore only be a small, fast cache; raw silicon works out to ~$65–110 per GBD.

DRAM一个晶体管加一个电容One transistor plus one capacitor

电荷会漏，所以要不停刷新；电容必须做得又细又深，长鑫 G5 的存储电容深宽比约 45:1、单元阵列高约 6.8 µmS247B。微缩越来越难（SK 海力士 CTO：靠现有技术提升性能和容量"越来越难"S230A），下一步是 4F² 垂直沟道（三星 10a，2028 年量产目标）和 3D DRAM（约 2030 年后）S222B。HBM、DDR5、LPDDR、GDDR 都是同一种 DRAM 晶圆的不同"封装与接口"。Charge leaks, so cells are refreshed constantly, and the capacitor must be thin and deep: CXMT's G5 capacitor has an aspect ratio of ~45:1 in a ~6.8 µm cell arrayS247B. Scaling keeps getting harder (SK hynix's CTO: "increasingly difficult" with current technologyS230A); next come 4F² vertical-channel cells (Samsung 10a, targeting 2028) and 3D DRAM (around 2030 or later)S222B. HBM, DDR5, LPDDR and GDDR are the same DRAM wafers in different packages and interfaces.

NAND把电荷锁进三维塔楼Charge locked in a 3D tower

断电不丢、密度最高，但按页读、按块擦，每个单元只能擦写有限次数，写入比读取慢得多。竞争集中在堆叠层数与密度：三星 V10 超过 400 层、铠侠/闪迪 BiCS10 为 332 层（QLC 37 Gb/mm²）S276S277B。增加层数需要深孔刻蚀与晶圆键合，所以 NAND 的 bit 增长主要靠工艺迁移而非新建厂房S212B。Persistent and the densest, but it reads by page, erases by block, endures a limited number of program/erase cycles and writes far slower than it reads. Competition is about layers and density: Samsung's V10 exceeds 400 layers, Kioxia/SanDisk's BiCS10 has 332 (QLC at 37 Gb/mm²)S276S277B. More layers need deep-hole etch and wafer bonding, so NAND bit growth comes mainly from process migration rather than new fabsS212B.

HDD 磁带Tape磁性颗粒与机械Magnetic grains and mechanics

容量增长靠"台数 × 每台碟片数 × 每碟容量"：希捷 HAMR Mozaic 4+ 为 10 碟 44 TB（每碟 4.4 TB）S316A。随机访问要几毫秒，因此只适合顺序吞吐与冷数据。磁带访问以秒到分钟计，LTO-10 单盘原生 30–40 TB，成本最低S301A。Capacity grows as units × platters per drive × TB per platter: Seagate's HAMR Mozaic 4+ packs 44 TB on 10 platters (4.4 TB each)S316A. Random access takes milliseconds, so disks suit sequential throughput and cold data. Tape access is seconds to minutes; an LTO-10 cartridge holds 30–40 TB native at the lowest costS301A.

AI 改写层级的四个结构性变化Four structural changes AI has made to the hierarchy

  1. HBM 成了加速器的"主存"，DRAM 产业的重心搬进了 GPU 封装。HBM became the accelerator's main memory, and the DRAM industry's center of gravity moved into the GPU package. HBM 占 DRAM 晶圆投入的比例从约 18%（2025）升至 22%（2026）和 30%（2027）S167B；HBM 已超过 GPU 物料成本的 50%S191C。HBM's share of DRAM wafer input rises from ~18% (2025) to 22% (2026) and 30% (2027)S167B; HBM now exceeds 50% of GPU bill of materialsS191C.
  2. CPU 内存变成了 GPU 内存的一层。CPU memory became a layer of GPU memory. NVLink-C2C 让 Grace（900 GB/s）与 Vera（1.8 TB/s）的 LPDDR5X 直接充当 KV 外溢层；Vera 每颗最多 1.5 TB，NVL72 机架最多 54 TBS41S46S27A。CPU:GPU 配比从 1:8 走向 1:4 乃至 1:2S87B。NVLink-C2C lets the LPDDR5X behind Grace (900 GB/s) and Vera (1.8 TB/s) act as a KV spill tier; each Vera takes up to 1.5 TB, and an NVL72 rack up to 54 TBS41S46S27A. CPU:GPU ratios move from 1:8 towards 1:4 or 1:2S87B.
  3. 闪存往上走了一层。Flash moved up a tier. KV 缓存开始常驻闪存：NVIDIA 在本地 SSD（G3）与共享存储（G4）之间新增了以太网连接的"G3.5"上下文闪存层（CMX）S30A；企业级 SSD 在 2Q26 已占 NAND bit 出货的 48%（一年前 26%）S273B；HBF 则试图把 NAND 放进封装。The KV cache now lives on flash: NVIDIA added an Ethernet-attached "G3.5" context-flash tier (CMX) between local SSD (G3) and shared storage (G4)S30A; enterprise SSDs took 48% of NAND bits in 2Q26, up from 26% a year earlierS273B; HBF aims to put NAND inside the package.
  4. 存储变成了推理性能部件。Storage became an inference-performance component. KV 命中率直接决定首 token 延迟与成本：DeepSeek 用磁盘缓存把 128K 提示的首 token 延迟从 13 秒降到 0.5 秒S24A，llm-d 的缓存感知调度让 P90 首 token 延迟快 57 倍S77A。VAST、WEKA、DDN、Everpure、NetApp、华为都在卖"上下文内存"产品S304S305S306S308S309S307。KV hit rates set time-to-first-token and cost: DeepSeek's disk cache cut first-token latency on a 128K prompt from 13 s to 0.5 sS24A, and llm-d's cache-aware scheduling made P90 TTFT 57× fasterS77A. VAST, WEKA, DDN, Everpure, NetApp and Huawei all now sell "context memory" productsS304S305S306S308S309S307.

SRAM

02

SRAM：最快的一层，也是最小、最贵的一层SRAM: the fastest tier, and the smallest and costliest

负责什么Role寄存器、L1/共享内存、L2；FlashAttention 的分块暂存；SRAM 派芯片里直接存放权重Registers, L1/shared memory, L2; FlashAttention tile scratch; weights in SRAM-centric chips
关键特性Traits~1–30 ns；单芯片数十至 150 TB/s；易失、无需刷新；密度最低~1–30 ns; tens to 150 TB/s per chip; volatile, no refresh; lowest density
制造壁垒Barrier与逻辑同工艺：壁垒是先进逻辑制程（N3/N2、SF4X）与 SRAM 宏设计，不是独立的存储工厂Same process as logic: the barrier is leading-edge logic (N3/N2, SF4X) and SRAM macro design, not a separate memory fab
难度Difficulty （随逻辑工艺）(rides on logic)
用量Volume每颗 50–500 MB；晶圆级 44 GB；LPX 机架 128 GB50–500 MB per chip; 44 GB per wafer-scale chip; 128 GB per LPX rack
供需Supply受先进晶圆与 CoWoS 产能约束；SRAM 面积与计算面积互相争抢Bound by leading-edge wafers and CoWoS; SRAM area competes with compute area

主要玩家Players台积电、三星、英特尔代工；NVIDIA（Groq LPX）、Cerebras、谷歌、AMD、d-MatrixTSMC, Samsung, Intel foundries; NVIDIA (Groq LPX), Cerebras, Google, AMD, d-Matrix

SRAM 在 AI 芯片里做什么What SRAM does inside an AI chip

GPU 的每个计算单元（SM）都有寄存器和 L1/共享内存：H100 每个 SM 有 256 KB 寄存器文件与 256 KB L1/共享内存，L2 为 50 MB；B200 的 L2 增至 126 MB，并新增 256 KB 的张量内存S48A。FlashAttention 这类内核的核心思路就是把注意力计算切成小块放进 SRAM 里反复使用，少读写 HBM。另一条路线是"SRAM 派"芯片：AMD MI355X 有 256 MB Infinity Cache，微软 Maia 200 有 272 MB SRAM，谷歌 TPU 8i 有 384 MB SRAM（按"推理模型的 KV 占用"设计），NVIDIA 的 Groq 3 LP30 每颗 500 MB、带宽 150 TB/s，Cerebras WSE-3 一整片晶圆 44 GBS64S69S66S31S71A。Each GPU streaming multiprocessor has registers and L1/shared memory: H100 has a 256 KB register file and 256 KB of L1/shared memory per SM, plus 50 MB of L2; B200 raises L2 to 126 MB and adds 256 KB of tensor memoryS48A. Kernels such as FlashAttention work by tiling attention into SRAM-sized blocks to cut HBM traffic. The other route is SRAM-centric chips: AMD's MI355X carries 256 MB of Infinity Cache, Microsoft's Maia 200 272 MB of SRAM, Google's TPU 8i 384 MB (sized for reasoning models' KV footprint), NVIDIA's Groq 3 LP30 500 MB at 150 TB/s, and a Cerebras WSE-3 wafer 44 GBS64S69S66S31S71A.

每颗芯片的片上 SRAM（对数刻度）On-chip SRAM per chip (log scale)
GPU 的 SRAM 是缓存；SRAM 派芯片把它当主存。d-Matrix 为每卡，Cerebras 为每片晶圆。In GPUs SRAM is cache; SRAM-centric chips use it as main memory. d-Matrix is per card, Cerebras per wafer.

为什么 SRAM 做不大：微缩停滞与成本Why SRAM cannot get big: stalled scaling and cost

工艺节点Node高密度单元面积HD bitcell密度Density
TSMC N50.021 µm²~31.8 Mib/mm²

TSMC N20.0175–0.021 µm²~38 Mb/mm²
Intel 18A0.021 µm²31.8–38.1 Mb/mm²

N5→N3E 逻辑密度提升 1.6–1.7 倍，SRAM 却原地踏步S198B；N2 约提升 10%S199B。N2 的单元面积各源说法不一C。Logic density rose 1.6–1.7× from N5 to N3E while SRAM stood stillS198B; N2 adds ~10%S199B. Sources disagree on N2's cell areaC.

算一笔账The arithmetic

一片 300mm 晶圆约 70,686 mm²。N3E 的 SRAM 密度约 31.8 Mib/mm²，整片约能放 274 GiB；按 1.8–2 万美元/片折算约 66–73 美元/GiB，扣掉外围电路后约 77–86 美元。N2 晶圆约 3 万美元，约 108 美元/GiBD。对比 2026 年 HBM4 约 16–17 美元/GB、2027 年约 36 美元/GBS186B：SRAM 每 GB 贵 2–9 倍，但每 GB 的带宽高约 4,300 倍（150 TB/s÷0.5 GB 对比 ~20 TB/s÷288 GB）D。A 300mm wafer has ~70,686 mm². At ~31.8 Mib/mm², N3E fits ~274 GiB of SRAM per wafer; at $18–20K per wafer that is ~$66–73/GiB, or ~$77–86 after peripheral overhead. An N2 wafer at ~$30K gives ~$108/GiBD. Against HBM4 at ~$16–17/GB in 2026 and ~$36/GB in 2027S186B, SRAM costs 2–9× more per GB but delivers ~4,300× more bandwidth per GB (150 TB/s ÷ 0.5 GB versus ~20 TB/s ÷ 288 GB)D.

SRAM 派推理芯片：优势、代价与 NVIDIA 的用法SRAM-centric inference chips: strengths, costs and how NVIDIA uses them

来源：S31S32S55S56S57S200S71S72S202S203S59Sources: S31S32S55S56S57S200S71S72S202S203S59

NVIDIA 的做法说明了 SRAM 的边界。Groq 3 LPX 与 Vera Rubin NVL72 采用"注意力与 FFN 分离"（AFD）：有状态、需要 KV 缓存的注意力留在 Rubin GPU 的 HBM 上，无状态的 FFN/MoE 层放到 LPU 上流式计算；NVIDIA 宣称在每用户 400 token/s 的交互速度下，单位兆瓦吞吐是 GB200 NVL72 的 35 倍S31A。这是一个厂商口径，但结构很清楚：SRAM 解决的是"权重流式读取的带宽"，不解决"上下文的容量"。一颗 Rubin 的 288 GB HBM 约等于 2.25 个 LPX 机架的全部 SRAM；一个约 1.6T 参数、按约 4.5 bit 存储（约 0.9 TB）的模型，仅放权重就要约 7 个 LPX 机架D。NVIDIA's design shows where SRAM stops. Groq 3 LPX pairs with Vera Rubin NVL72 through attention–FFN disaggregation: stateful attention, which needs the KV cache, stays in Rubin's HBM, while stateless FFN/MoE layers stream through the LPUs. NVIDIA claims 35× the throughput per megawatt of GB200 NVL72 at 400 tokens/s per userS31A. That is a vendor claim, but the structure is clear: SRAM solves weight-streaming bandwidth, not context capacity. One Rubin's 288 GB of HBM equals all the SRAM in ~2.25 LPX racks; a ~1.6T-parameter model stored at ~4.5 bits (~0.9 TB) needs ~7 LPX racks for its weights aloneD.

SRAM 的演进方向Where SRAM is heading

  • 向上堆叠：用混合键合把 SRAM 叠在计算裸片上（类似 3D V-Cache），绕开平面面积约束。下一代 Groq LP40 计划转到台积电 N3P + CoWoS-R，并加入 SK 海力士的混合键合 DRAMS57B——连 SRAM 派也在往上叠 DRAM。Stack it: hybrid-bond SRAM onto compute (as with 3D V-Cache) to escape planar area limits. The next Groq LP40 moves to TSMC N3P with CoWoS-R and adds hybrid-bonded SK hynix DRAMS57B: even the SRAM camp is stacking DRAM on top.
  • 新材料替代：SOT-MRAM 已做到 1 ns 翻转、10 年以上保持（64Kb 阵列），被视为缓存候选，但最早要到本十年后期S139B。New materials: SOT-MRAM has shown 1 ns switching and 10+ year retention in a 64Kb array and is a cache candidate, but not before late this decadeS139B.
  • 存内计算：台积电在 VLSI 2026 展示 2nm 数字存内计算，能效 234.4 TOPS/WS130A；IBM NorthPole 无片外存储，3B 模型单 token 延迟低于 1 msS129A。目前限于小模型与边缘。Compute-in-memory: TSMC showed 2nm digital CIM at 234.4 TOPS/W at VLSI 2026S130A; IBM's NorthPole, with no off-chip memory, ran a 3B model at under 1 ms per tokenS129A. Both remain limited to small models and the edge.
判断Judgment

SRAM 是 HBM 的互补品而非替代品。SRAM 派芯片能显著提升每用户的 token 速度，却把 KV 缓存留给了 HBM；它扩大的是"高交互推理"这个市场，最终仍需要更多的 HBM 与（LP40 起）堆叠 DRAM。SRAM complements HBM rather than replacing it. SRAM-centric chips sharply raise tokens per second per user but leave the KV cache to HBM; they grow the market for highly interactive inference, which still needs more HBM and, from LP40 on, stacked DRAM.

HBM

03

HBM：AI 时代最稀缺、最难造的存储HBM: the scarcest and hardest-to-make memory of the AI era

HBM 是什么：一张剖面图What HBM is: one cross-section

HBM 把多颗 DRAM 裸片用硅通孔（TSV）垂直串起来，叠在一颗负责接口与控制的基底裸片上，再和 GPU 一起放在硅中介层上（台积电 CoWoS）。超宽的接口（HBM4 每堆栈 2,048 位）配合很短的走线，是它带宽远高于 DDR5 的原因；代价是每一步都比普通 DRAM 难。HBM links several DRAM dies vertically with through-silicon vias (TSVs), stacks them on a base die that handles the interface and control, and sits next to the GPU on a silicon interposer (TSMC CoWoS). A very wide interface (2,048 bits per stack in HBM4) over very short wires is why its bandwidth dwarfs DDR5; the price is that every step is harder than ordinary DRAM.

HBM 与 GPU 的封装剖面（示意，不按比例）HBM and GPU package cross-section (schematic, not to scale)
堆叠高度上限：HBM3E 720 µm、HBM4 775 µm，HBM4E 据报放宽至约 900 µmS156S174；单颗厚度为 775 µm÷16 层的量级推算D。基底裸片工艺见S172S171。Height caps: 720 µm (HBM3E), 775 µm (HBM4), reportedly ~900 µm for HBM4ES156S174; per-die thickness is an order-of-magnitude estimate from 775 µm ÷ 16 layersD. Base-die processes perS172S171.

为什么 HBM 这么难造：八道关Why HBM is so hard to make: eight gates

  1. 先进 DRAM 节点。Leading DRAM node. HBM4 用 1b/1c/1γ 等最新节点；SK 海力士 1c 用 6 层 EUV，三星 1c 用 6–7 层（从计划的 8–9 层下调），美光 1γ 只用 1 层 EUVS261S262BC。三星 1c 良率 2026 年 2 月约 60%，下半年超过 80%S170B。HBM4 uses the newest nodes (1b/1c/1γ). SK hynix's 1c uses 6 EUV layers, Samsung's 1c 6–7 (cut from a planned 8–9), Micron's 1γ only oneS261S262BC. Samsung's 1c yield was ~60% in Feb 2026 and above 80% later in the yearS170B.
  2. 更大的裸片、更低的良率、更长的周期。Bigger dies, lower yield, longer cycle. 同节点同容量下，HBM 裸片比 DDR5 大 35–45%，良率低 20–30%，生产周期长 1.5–2 个月S168B；HBM4 的 2,048 个 I/O 让裸片更大，从晶圆到成品超过 6 个月。At the same node and capacity an HBM die is 35–45% larger than DDR5, yields 20–30% lower and takes 1.5–2 months longerS168B; HBM4's 2,048 I/Os enlarge the die further, and wafer-to-package takes more than six months.
  3. TSV 与晶圆减薄。TSVs and wafer thinning. 每颗 DRAM 裸片要打上千个硅通孔，再减薄到数十微米；整个堆栈必须塞进 775 µm（HBM4）的高度上限。行业 TSV 产能约 21.6 万片/月（2024）→ 35 万（2025），Yole 预计 2030 年 59 万S189B。Each DRAM die gets thousands of TSVs and is thinned to tens of microns; the whole stack must fit under HBM4's 775 µm cap. Industry TSV capacity went from ~216K wafers/month (2024) to 350K (2025), and Yole sees 590K by 2030S189B.
  4. 堆叠键合三条路线。Three stacking routes. SK 海力士用 Advanced MR-MUF（回流焊+模塑填充），三星与美光用 TC-NCF（热压+非导电膜）。混合键合把凸点间距从约 20 µm 压到 1 µm 以下，散热好 100 倍以上、密度 15 倍，但成本是倒装的 2–3 倍S156A；SK 海力士已把它推迟到 HBM5，三星计划在 P5 装约 50 台混合键合机、2030 年量产S194S184B。SK hynix uses Advanced MR-MUF (reflow plus molded underfill); Samsung and Micron use TC-NCF (thermo-compression with non-conductive film). Hybrid bonding cuts the pitch from ~20 µm to under 1 µm with >100× better heat removal and 15× density, at 2–3× the cost of flip-chipS156A; SK hynix has pushed it to HBM5, and Samsung plans ~50 hybrid bonders at P5 for volume in 2030S194S184B.
  5. 基底裸片变成逻辑芯片。The base die becomes a logic chip. HBM4 起基底裸片转向晶圆代工逻辑工艺：台积电 N12（电压 1.1→0.8 V）与定制 C-HBM4E 的 N3P（内存控制器与 PHY 移入基底）S172B。台积电基底据报成本是 SK 海力士自研核心裸片的 3–4 倍S173B；三星用自家 4nm，涨价 40–50% 且产能排满，并把平泽一条 3 万片/月代工线的一半以上用于 HBM4 基底S171S379S378B。From HBM4 the base die moves to foundry logic: TSMC N12 (1.1→0.8 V) and N3P for custom C-HBM4E, with the memory controller and PHY moved into the baseS172B. TSMC base dies reportedly cost 3–4× SK hynix's own core diesS173B; Samsung uses its own 4nm, raised prices 40–50% with the line fully booked, and gave over half of a 30K-wafer/month Pyeongtaek foundry line to HBM4 base diesS171S379S378B.
  6. 已知良品（KGD）与系统级测试。Known-good die and system-level test. 一颗坏裸片会废掉整个堆栈乃至整颗 GPU。SK 海力士自建与客户芯片联测的 HBM4 系统级测试设备；爱德万占存储测试 50% 以上，泰瑞达约占服务器内存测试 30%S180B。One bad die scraps a whole stack, or a whole GPU. SK hynix built HBM4 system-level test gear that tests with the customer's chip; Advantest holds over 50% of memory test, and Teradyne ~30% of server-memory testS180B.
  7. 散热与层数。Heat and layer count. 16 层仍在客户认证，12 层仍是 HBM4E 主流S175S194B；SK 海力士 iHBM 方案将热阻降低 30% 以上，三星提出 zHBM（把 HBM 直接叠在计算芯片上）S374B。16-high is still in customer qualification and 12-high remains mainstream for HBM4ES175S194B; SK hynix's iHBM cuts thermal resistance by over 30%, and Samsung proposes zHBM, stacking HBM directly on computeS374B.
  8. 与 GPU 厂商联合设计与认证。Co-design and qualification with the GPU maker. NVIDIA 曾要求 HBM4 单引脚超过 11 Gbps，导致量产推迟S375B；Rubin 带宽口径从 22 TB/s 被放宽到 19.2 TB/s（产品页）S27S60AC。三星 2026-02-12 才首发 HBM4 商用出货，6 月黄仁勋表示三家都已完成 HBM4 认证S160S179AB。NVIDIA pushed for HBM4 pin speeds above 11 Gbps, delaying volume productionS375B; Rubin's bandwidth spec was relaxed from 22 TB/s to 19.2 TB/s on the product pageS27S60AC. Samsung made its first commercial HBM4 shipment only on Feb 12, 2026, and in June Jensen Huang said all three makers had qualified HBM4S160S179AB.
HBM 吃掉的晶圆远多于它产出的 bitHBM consumes far more wafers than the bits it yields
TrendForce：HBM 占 DRAM 晶圆投入与 bit 产出的比例；折线为推算的"每 bit 晶圆倍数"（右轴）TrendForce: HBM's share of DRAM wafer input and of bit output; the line is the implied wafer-per-bit multiple (right axis)
数据表Data table

Micron cites a 3:1 trade ratio with DDR5 that rises with each generationS164A; Samsung says ~3×S177B; on a per-GB basis TrendForce has said 4×S368B.

客户结构正在从 NVIDIA 一家独大转向 ASIC 分流。TrendForce：NVIDIA 占 HBM 需求 61%（2024）与 68%（2025F）S169B。到 2027 年，摩根士丹利估 NVIDIA 37.3%、谷歌 36%、AMD 12.1%S191C；JPMorgan 估 ASIC 48% 对 NVIDIA 43%S190B。这意味着 HBM 的议价对象更分散，但也意味着定制基底裸片（每家 ASIC 一套逻辑）会让产品更碎片化、更难互换。The customer base is shifting from NVIDIA alone towards ASICs. TrendForce: NVIDIA took 61% of HBM demand in 2024 and 68% in 2025FS169B. For 2027, Morgan Stanley sees NVIDIA at 37.3%, Google 36% and AMD 12.1%S191C; JPMorgan sees ASICs at 48% versus NVIDIA's 43%S190B. Pricing power faces more buyers, but custom base dies (one logic design per ASIC) make products more fragmented and less interchangeable.

价格：2026 年"暂停"，2027 年重置Prices: a 2026 pause and a 2027 reset

每 GB 价格：HBM 与通用 DRAM 已经"倒挂"Price per GB: HBM and commodity DRAM have inverted
美元/GB。长约价与现货价相差 4–5 倍；通用服务器 DRAM 的每 GB 价格已接近甚至高于 2026 年的 HBM 合约价。US$/GB. LTA and spot prices differ by 4–5×; commodity server DRAM now costs about as much per GB as 2026 HBM contracts, or more.
数据表Data table
换算：1 GB = 8 Gb。来源：S176S186S187S190S255S253；Bernstein 53 美元/GB 为转述C。Conversion: 1 GB = 8 Gb. Sources: S176S186S187S190S255S253; Bernstein's $53/GB is a relayC.D

为什么会倒挂？HBM 多按年度合约定价，而通用 DRAM 在 2025 年底到 2026 年上半年随现货与季度合约暴涨：TrendForce 指出 1Q26 一片晶圆做 DDR5 64GB RDIMM 的收入已超过做 HBMS167B。这给了厂商把晶圆从 HBM 挪回通用 DRAM 的动机，于是 2027 年 HBM 必须重新定价：TrendForce 预计混合均价 +121%，HBM4 12 层 36GB 从约 600 美元涨到约 1,300 美元S166S186B；其他机构预测 +54%（JPM）到 +79%（UBS）S190S182B。叠加 2027 年 bit 出货 +50–60%，2027 年 HBM 营收可能是 2026 年的 3.3–3.5 倍D。美光已以"显著的同比涨幅"签下 2027 年绝大部分 HBM bitS161A。Why the inversion? HBM is priced mostly on annual contracts, while commodity DRAM soared on spot and quarterly contracts from late 2025 into 1H26. TrendForce noted that in 1Q26 a wafer of DDR5 64GB RDIMM earned more than a wafer of HBMS167B. That gave suppliers a reason to move wafers back to commodity DRAM, so 2027 HBM must reprice: TrendForce sees the blended ASP up 121%, with a 12-high 36GB HBM4 stack going from ~$600 to ~$1,300S166S186B; others forecast +54% (JPM) to +79% (UBS)S190S182B. With 2027 bit shipments up 50–60%, 2027 HBM revenue could be 3.3–3.5× 2026D. Micron has contracted "the vast majority" of its 2027 HBM bits at "significant" year-on-year increasesS161A.

HBM 挤出效应计算器：HBM 多吃一点晶圆，通用 DRAM 少多少？HBM crowding-out calculator: when HBM takes more wafers, how much commodity DRAM is lost?
默认值按 TrendForce 晶圆/bit 份额与美光 2027 年行业 bit 增速（low-20s%）校准；改动任一输入即可看到 2027 年非 HBM 的 bit 供给增速与缺口。Defaults are calibrated to TrendForce's wafer/bit shares and Micron's low-20s% 2027 industry bit growth; change any input to see 2027 non-HBM bit supply growth and the gap.
预设Presets
含新洁净室与转产Includes new cleanrooms and conversions
HBM4/4E 裸片更大，提升较慢HBM4/4E dies are larger, so gains are slower
需求超出供给的比例（JPM 20%）Demand above supply (JPM: 20%)

口径：把 2026 年每片通用晶圆的 bit 记为 1，HBM 每片晶圆 bit = 1 ÷ 倍数。2027 年各类 bit = 晶圆份额 × 晶圆增速 × 各自每片 bit 提升。缺口 = 供给 ÷ 需求 − 1。默认下非 HBM 供给 +15.6%，与 TrendForce"RDIMM 2027 年 bit 供给 +15–20%"及韩华"2027 年 −5.6%"的口径相近S213S251。这是示意模型，与《缺口的算术》v4.2 的完整口径不同，数字冲突时以你的 Excel 为准。Basis: a 2026 commodity wafer yields 1 unit of bits; an HBM wafer yields 1 ÷ multiple. 2027 bits = wafer share × wafer growth × that type's bits-per-wafer gain. Gap = supply ÷ demand − 1. With defaults non-HBM supply grows 15.6%, close to TrendForce's "RDIMM bit supply +15–20% in 2027" and Hanwha's "−5.6% in 2027"S213S251. This is an illustrative model; it differs from the full basis of your Gap Arithmetic v4.2, and your Excel prevails where numbers conflict.D

HBM 往哪里走Where HBM is heading

更快：HBM4E 与定制基底Faster: HBM4E and custom base dies

2027 年 HBM4E（14–16 Gbps）放量；基底裸片内置内存控制器、近存计算（三星规划的第二、三阶段：控制器上移 → 近存计算 → HBM 直接叠在计算裸片上）S132B。把内存控制器（约占 GPU 面积 5–10%）挪进基底，可提升 10–20% 性能S374B。HBM4E (14–16 Gbps) ramps in 2027. Base dies take on the memory controller and near-memory compute (Samsung's phases: controller moves down → near-memory compute → HBM stacked on compute)S132B. Moving the memory controller (5–10% of GPU area) into the base die is said to add 10–20% performanceS374B.

更高：16 层到 20 层以上Taller: 16 to 20+ layers

JEDEC 据报将 HBM4E 高度放宽到约 900 µm，推迟了混合键合的必要性；20 层以上（HBM5 代）才全面需要混合键合S174S156BA。混合键合设备（BESI、应用材料、韩华、SEMES、韩美）是下一个增量S193S195。JEDEC reportedly relaxes the HBM4E height to ~900 µm, delaying the need for hybrid bonding; 20+ layers (HBM5) will require itS174S156BA. Hybrid-bonding tools (BESI, Applied Materials, Hanwha, SEMES, Hanmi) are the next incrementS193S195.

更远：SPHBM4 与光互连Farther: SPHBM4 and optics

SPHBM4 用串行化接口与有机基板，让一颗芯片周围能放更多堆栈、走线更长S152A；Marvell 收购 Celestial AI 后称光互连可让设计者在封装内放"显著更多"的 HBMS124A。SPHBM4 uses a serialized interface on organic substrates so more stacks can surround a chip over longer tracesS152A; after buying Celestial AI, Marvell says optical links let designers fit "significantly" more HBM per packageS124A.

更便宜的替代层：HBFA cheaper adjacent tier: HBF

高带宽闪存用 NAND 堆叠提供接近 HBM 的带宽与 8–16 倍容量，主打读多写少的推理权重；首版 OCP 规范已发布，SanDisk 目标 2027 送样、2028 量产，NVIDIA 尚未承诺采用S135S134AB。详见 §06。High Bandwidth Flash stacks NAND for near-HBM bandwidth and 8–16× capacity, aimed at read-mostly inference weights. The first OCP spec is out; SanDisk targets samples in 2027 and volume in 2028; NVIDIA has not committedS135S134AB. See §06.

▲支撑 HBM 的力量What supports HBM

  1. 每代加速器容量与带宽持续上升（MI455X 432 GB、MTIA 500 最高 512 GB）S63S70。Capacity and bandwidth per accelerator keep rising (MI455X 432 GB, MTIA 500 up to 512 GB)S63S70.
  2. ASIC 需求快速增长，客户分散S190。Fast-growing ASIC demand from more buyersS190.
  3. RL rollout、长上下文与 Agent 都是 decode 密集型，直接吃 HBM 带宽与容量（§09–§11）。RL rollouts, long context and agents are decode-heavy and consume HBM bandwidth and capacity directly (§09–§11).
  4. 每 bit 晶圆倍数与长约锁定限制了供给弹性S167S185。Wafer intensity and LTAs limit supply elasticityS167S185.

▼压制 HBM 的力量What weighs on HBM

  1. 买家减配：8 层堆栈、Rubin Ultra 降配、规格放宽S166S377S27。Buyers trim: 8-high stacks, a smaller Rubin Ultra, relaxed specsS166S377S27.
  2. KV 压缩（MLA、CSA2、FP4 KV）降低每会话的 HBM 占用S95。KV compression (MLA, CSA2, FP4 KV) cuts HBM per sessionS95.
  3. 三星份额 21%→33%，2028 年后可能出现价格竞争S188。Samsung's share rose 21%→33%; price competition may follow after 2028S188.
  4. 价值部分流向台积电基底与 CoWoSS172S173。Part of the value flows to TSMC base dies and CoWoSS172S173.

DRAM（DDR5 / LPDDR5X·SOCAMM2 / MRDIMM / GDDR7）DRAM (DDR5 / LPDDR5X·SOCAMM2 / MRDIMM / GDDR7)

04

DRAM 主存：被 HBM 挤压、又被 Agent 重新需要的一层Main memory (DRAM): squeezed by HBM, then needed again by agents

负责什么RoleCPU 主存；KV 外溢第二层（G2）；数据预处理、工具执行、强化学习环境、内存内 checkpoint；GDDR7 用于图形与低成本推理卡CPU memory; KV spill tier G2; data prep, tool execution, RL environments, in-memory checkpoints; GDDR7 for graphics and low-cost inference cards
关键特性Traits~80–150 ns；DDR5 每插槽约 0.8–1.6 TB/s（16 通道）；Vera LPDDR5X 1.2 TB/s；单 CPU 0.5–2 TB~80–150 ns; ~0.8–1.6 TB/s per DDR5 socket (16 channels); 1.2 TB/s for Vera LPDDR5X; 0.5–2 TB per CPU

主要玩家Players三星 39%、SK 海力士 25%、美光 23%（2Q26）；长鑫约 7–9%；南亚、华邦Samsung 39%, SK hynix 25%, Micron 23% (2Q26); CXMT ~7–9%; Nanya, WinbondS207S256

同一种晶圆，五种产品One wafer, five products

HBM、DDR5、LPDDR、GDDR 都来自同一类 DRAM 晶圆，区别在于封装、接口与可靠性设计。这一点决定了 AI 对 DRAM 的影响是"全品类"的：HBM 多吃一片晶圆，DDR5 与 LPDDR 就少一片。HBM, DDR5, LPDDR and GDDR all come from the same kind of DRAM wafer; they differ in package, interface and reliability design. So AI's effect on DRAM spans every product: each wafer HBM takes is one less for DDR5 and LPDDR.

为什么 AI 又开始大量需要 CPU 内存Why AI needs lots of CPU memory again

  • KV 外溢第二层：GB200/GB300 的 Grace 经 NVLink-C2C（900 GB/s，约为 PCIe Gen5 的 7 倍）把 LPDDR5X 变成 GPU 可直接读写的扩展内存；NVIDIA 测得 GH200 上 CPU 卸载比 x86-H100 重算快最多 14 倍S41A。Vera 把 C2C 提到 1.8 TB/sS46A。KV spill tier two: Grace in GB200/GB300 turns LPDDR5X into memory the GPU reads directly over NVLink-C2C (900 GB/s, ~7× PCIe Gen5); NVIDIA measured CPU offload on GH200 up to 14× faster than recompute on x86-H100S41A. Vera doubles C2C to 1.8 TB/sS46A.
  • Agent 工具执行：浏览器、代码沙箱、检索与编排都跑在 CPU 上；TrendForce 称 CPU:GPU 比例从 1:8 走向 1:4 或更高，NVL72 已是 1:2S87B。NVIDIA 的 Vera CPU 机架最多 256 颗 CPU、400 TB 内存S46S385AB。Agent tool execution: browsers, code sandboxes, retrieval and orchestration run on CPUs; TrendForce says CPU:GPU ratios are moving from 1:8 to 1:4 or higher, and NVL72 is already 1:2S87B. NVIDIA's Vera CPU rack holds up to 256 CPUs and 400 TB of memoryS46S385AB.
  • 数据与容错：训练的数据预处理是 CPU+内存密集型；Gemini 用内存内冗余副本替代从持久存储恢复，把最大任务的 goodput 从 85% 提到 97%S8A。Data and fault tolerance: training data prep is CPU- and memory-heavy; Gemini kept redundant in-memory replicas instead of restoring from persistent storage, lifting goodput on its largest job from 85% to 97%S8A.

电容：越做越深The capacitor keeps getting deeper

每个单元的电容必须在几十纳米宽的面积里存住足够电荷，于是只能向上长。长鑫 G5 的深宽比约 45:1，单元阵列高约 6,762 nmS247B。4F² 要用 IGZO 沟道降低漏电、用钼字线，还要单独做一片外围逻辑晶圆再键合S222B。Each cell's capacitor must hold enough charge in a footprint tens of nanometres wide, so it grows upward. CXMT's G5 has a ~45:1 aspect ratio in a ~6,762 nm cell arrayS247B. 4F² needs IGZO channels to cut leakage, molybdenum word lines, and a separate peripheral-logic wafer bonded onS222B.

洁净室：真正的瓶颈Cleanrooms: the real bottleneck

TrendForce：DRAM 行业洁净室产能有限，只有三星与 SK 海力士还能小幅扩张；新厂 2H27 才开始有意义爬坡，2028 年才有大量产出S216S212B。美光 FY27 资本开支增量主要用于 2028 年底以后投产的洁净室S161A。TrendForce: DRAM cleanroom capacity is limited, and only Samsung and SK hynix can expand slightly; new fabs ramp meaningfully from 2H27 with substantial output only in 2028S216S212B. Micron's FY27 capex increase is mostly cleanroom construction for capacity from late 2028S161A.

资本强度Capital intensity

按南亚 Fab 5A 推算，每月每片投片能力约需 30–36 万美元（含厂房与设备）S260D。行业 DRAM 资本开支从 2025 年 537 亿美元增至 2026 年约 613 亿美元（2025 年 11 月估计，此后多家上调）S216B。From Nanya's Fab 5A, each wafer-per-month of capacity costs ~$300–360K including shell and toolsS260D. Industry DRAM capex rises from $53.7bn (2025) to ~$61.3bn (2026), a Nov 2025 estimate since raised by several makersS216B.

KB 证券KB Securities—需求超出供给 10 个百分点以上Demand exceeds supply by >10 points三星与 SK 海力士库存低于 10 天（券商估计）Samsung/SK hynix inventory under 10 days (broker estimate)S252C
日经Nikkei厂商到 2027 年只能满足约 60% 的需求Makers can meet only ~60% of demand through 2027稳定不早于 2028 年Stabilization no earlier than 2028S364C

DRAM 的演进方向Where DRAM is heading

  • 更高密度颗粒与模组：32Gb 单体颗粒让 256GB RDIMM 与 256GB SOCAMM2 成为主流，512GB RDIMM 已送样S162S225A。Denser dies and modules: monolithic 32Gb dies make 256GB RDIMMs and 256GB SOCAMM2 mainstream, and 512GB RDIMMs are samplingS162S225A.
  • 更快的接口：LPDDR6（14.4 Gbps）、MRDIMM Gen3（16,000 MT/s，2H27）、DDR5-9200 时钟驱动器已量产（澜起 CKD）S153S242S240A。Faster interfaces: LPDDR6 (14.4 Gbps), MRDIMM Gen3 (16,000 MT/s, 2H27), and DDR5-9200 clock drivers already in volume (Montage CKD)S153S242S240A.
  • 存内处理：三星 LPDDR5X-PIM 原型实测矩阵向量运算快 2.28 倍，最早 2027 年量产；JEDEC LPDDR6-PIM 标准接近完成S128S153BA。Processing in memory: Samsung's LPDDR5X-PIM prototype measured 2.28× faster matrix-vector math, with volume possibly in 2027; the JEDEC LPDDR6-PIM standard is near completionS128S153BA.
  • 结构变革：4F² VCT（三星 10a，2028）、High-NA EUV（2028）、3D DRAM（约 2030 年以后），它们增加的是每片晶圆的 bit，即供给S222S223S130BA。Structural change: 4F² VCT (Samsung 10a, 2028), High-NA EUV (2028) and 3D DRAM (around 2030 or later) all add bits per wafer, which is supplyS222S223S130BA.
判断Judgment

通用 DRAM 的紧张不只是"HBM 挤出"的副作用，而是 AI 推理（尤其是 Agent）对 CPU 内存的真实新增需求。2027 年非 HBM 的 bit 供给只能增长约 15–20%，而需求增速在 20% 以上，缺口很可能延续到新洁净室在 2028 年放量（见上方 M5 模型）。最大的不确定性来自需求端：CSP 模组降配、NVIDIA 的 SOCAMM 减半，说明价格已经开始影响用量。Commodity DRAM tightness is not just a side effect of HBM crowding out; it reflects real new demand for CPU memory from inference, especially agents. In 2027 non-HBM bit supply can grow only ~15–20% against demand growth above 20%, so the gap likely lasts until new cleanrooms ramp in 2028 (see model M5 above). The biggest uncertainty is demand: CSP module downsizing and NVIDIA's halved SOCAMM show that price is already affecting volume.

CXL 内存CXL memory

05

CXL 与内存池化：把"闲着的内存"借给需要的人CXL and memory pooling: lending idle memory to whoever needs it

负责什么Role扩展或池化 DRAM；KV 缓存的"温"层；减少每台服务器被困住的内存；复用旧 DDR4Expand or pool DRAM; a warm KV tier; reduce memory stranded in each server; reuse old DDR4
关键特性Traits基于 PCIe 物理层的缓存一致性协议；比本地 DRAM 多约 100–250 ns；CXL 4.0 为 128 GT/sCache-coherent protocol on the PCIe PHY; ~100–250 ns above local DRAM; CXL 4.0 runs at 128 GT/sS236
制造壁垒Barrier控制器与交换芯片设计、互操作认证、软件分层；存储颗粒本身仍是 DRAMController and switch silicon, interoperability, tiering software; the media is still DRAM
难度Difficulty
用量Volume单交换机池最大 48 TB（Marvell）；CXL 实际启用服务器约 0% → 2030 年约 13%（Yole）Up to 48 TB behind one switch (Marvell); servers with CXL enabled ~0% → ~13% by 2030 (Yole)S123S244
供需Supply受 DRAM 供给约束；控制器不缺Bound by DRAM supply; controllers are not short
单价PriceDRAM 成本 + 控制器；池化可降低总 DRAM 成本约 7%（Azure Pond）DRAM cost plus controller; pooling cut total DRAM cost ~7% (Azure Pond)S122
主要玩家PlayersMarvell、Astera Labs、澜起、Microchip；三星/SK 海力士/美光模组Marvell, Astera Labs, Montage, Microchip; Samsung/SK hynix/Micron modules

CXL（Compute Express Link）让 CPU 或加速器通过 PCIe 物理链路访问另一块内存，并保持缓存一致。最常见的是 Type-3 内存扩展器（一张 E3.S 形态的卡上装 DDR5），再往上是 CXL 交换机，让多台主机共享一个内存池。它解决两个问题：单台服务器内存插槽不够，以及每台服务器各自留的内存经常闲置（"被困住"）。CXL (Compute Express Link) lets a CPU or accelerator reach other memory over a PCIe physical link while keeping caches coherent. The common device is a Type-3 memory expander (DDR5 on an E3.S card); above it, CXL switches let several hosts share one pool. It solves two problems: too few DIMM slots per server, and memory reserved in each server that sits idle ("stranded").

AI 推理实测AI inference results
三星白皮书Samsung white paper4 条 256GB CMM-D 组成 1 TB 池，作为 LMCache 后端：8 路张量并行时约达本地 DRAM 性能的 92%；缓存需求 700GB 超过本地 512GB 时，本地 DRAM 方案降到约 6.1 万 token/s，CXL 池维持约 35.4 万Four 256GB CMM-D (1 TB pool) as the LMCache backend: ~92% of local DRAM performance with 8-way TP; with a 700GB cache exceeding 512GB of local DRAM, the local setup fell to ~61K tokens/s while the CXL pool held ~354KS234
MarvellGPU 内存池化场景下推理吞吐最高 4.8 倍、首 token 延迟降低 82.7%Up to 4.8× inference throughput and 82.7% lower TTFT with pooled GPU memoryS123
美光（FMS 2026）Micron (FMS 2026)4U 内 40 TB 共享 DDR5，token/s 提升 6.7 倍、GPU 资源减少 85%（厂商口径）40 TB of shared DDR5 in 4U: 6.7× tokens/s with 85% fewer GPU resources (vendor claim)S245
Azure Pond8–16 个插槽池化可降低 DRAM 成本 7%，性能为本地内存的 1–5% 以内Pooling across 8–16 sockets cut DRAM cost 7% at within 1–5% of local performanceS122

为什么 CXL 还没有大规模起量Why CXL has not taken off yet

Yole 的数据很能说明问题：到 2026 年底超过 90% 的服务器"支持 CXL"，但真正启用 CXL 的服务器接近 0%，2030 年约 13%S244C。障碍有三：延迟比本地 DRAM 高一截，需要软件做冷热分层；NVIDIA 的 GPU 生态用 NVLink-C2C（Vera 1.8 TB/s）直接挂 LPDDR5X，CXL 主要活在 x86 与部分 ASIC 世界；最关键的是，CXL 并不创造新的 bit，内存紧缺时池化能"省"一些，但省不出产能。英特尔与 TechInsights 预计到 2031 年 49% 的服务器会采用内存池化S245B。Yole's numbers make the point: by end-2026 over 90% of servers are "CXL-capable", yet servers with CXL actually enabled are near 0%, rising to ~13% by 2030S244C. There are three obstacles. Latency is a notch above local DRAM, so software must tier hot and cold data. NVIDIA's GPU ecosystem attaches LPDDR5X directly over NVLink-C2C (1.8 TB/s on Vera), so CXL lives mostly in x86 and some ASIC systems. Most importantly, CXL creates no new bits: in a shortage pooling saves some memory but cannot save capacity. Intel and TechInsights expect memory pooling in 49% of servers by 2031S245B.

演进方向：从铜到光Direction: from copper to light

Marvell 以约 32.5 亿美元预付加最多 22.5 亿美元或有对价收购 Celestial AI，2026-02-02 完成，预计 FY2028 下半年开始贡献收入S124S125A。其 Photonic Fabric 设备可把 32 TB DDR5 组成统一内存，HBM 作为写直达缓存，并把"KV 缓存卸载"列为用途S126B；FMS 2026 上 Marvell 称可提供"最多 32 TB 的温 KV 缓存卸载"、吞吐提升 2–3 倍S123B。这条路线的本质仍是"让 DRAM 离 GPU 更近"，需求依旧落在 DRAM 上。Marvell bought Celestial AI for ~$3.25bn upfront plus up to $2.25bn in earnouts, closing on Feb 2, 2026, with revenue expected from 2H FY2028S124S125A. Its Photonic Fabric appliance pools 32 TB of DDR5 into unified memory with HBM as a write-through cache, and lists KV-cache offload as a use caseS126B; at FMS 2026 Marvell cited "up to 32 TB of warm KV cache offload" with 2–3× higher throughputS123B. The essence is still bringing DRAM closer to the GPU, so the demand still lands on DRAM.

判断Judgment

CXL 是"效率层"而不是"产能层"：它能提高 DRAM 的利用率、给 KV 缓存提供比闪存快得多的温层，并让旧 DDR4 再利用，但不会减少对 DRAM 的总需求。对产业链而言，受益的是控制器与交换芯片厂商；对存储需求而言，它是中性略偏正（更多内存被"挂"到推理系统里）。CXL is an efficiency layer, not a capacity layer. It raises DRAM utilization, gives the KV cache a warm tier far faster than flash and lets old DDR4 be reused, but it does not reduce total DRAM demand. In the value chain, controller and switch vendors benefit; for memory demand it is neutral to slightly positive, since more memory gets attached to inference systems.

NAND / 企业级 SSD / HBFNAND / enterprise SSD / HBF 存储级内存Storage-class memory

06

NAND 与企业级 SSD：KV 缓存把闪存往上推了一层NAND and enterprise SSD: the KV cache pushed flash up a tier

负责什么Role训练数据与 checkpoint、模型权重仓库与冷启动、KV 外溢（G3 本地/G3.5 机架/G4 共享）、向量库、替代 HDD 的温数据Training data and checkpoints, weight store and cold start, KV spill (G3 local / G3.5 rack / G4 shared), vector databases, warm data displacing HDD
关键特性Traits读 ~50–200 µs；PCIe 6.0 单盘 28 GB/s；单盘最大 245–512 TB；非易失；写入寿命有限~50–200 µs reads; 28 GB/s per PCIe 6.0 drive; up to 245–512 TB per drive; persistent; finite endurance

闪存在 AI 数据中心的六个岗位Six jobs flash does in AI data centers

① 训练数据与特征仓库① Training data and feature store

Meta 的 Tectonic 训练存储为 7,500 台 SSD 服务器上的 240 PB，持续 2 TB/s、峰值 7 TB/sS1A。文本加载带宽很低（见 §09），多模态与视频才需要高吞吐。Meta's Tectonic training storage is 240 PB on 7,500 SSD servers, sustaining 2 TB/s with 7 TB/s peaksS1A. Text loading needs little bandwidth (see §09); multimodal and video need throughput.

② Checkpoint 突发写② Checkpoint bursts

1T 参数的可重启 checkpoint 约 14 TB，必须在分钟以内写完；NVIDIA 建议写带宽至少为读带宽的一半S44AD。本地 NVMe 与并行文件系统承担这一层。A restartable checkpoint for 1T parameters is ~14 TB and must land within a minute or so; NVIDIA advises write bandwidth of at least half the read bandwidthS44AD. Local NVMe and parallel file systems take this job.

③ 权重仓库与冷启动③ Weight store and cold start

MoE 模型 FP8 权重约 0.7–1 TBS4S6D；vLLM 的分片权重传输在 48 个节点上 7.53 秒加载 Kimi K2S393A。模型越大、切换越频繁，越依赖高带宽 SSD。MoE weights in FP8 run ~0.7–1 TBS4S6D; vLLM's sharded weight transfer loaded Kimi K2 across 48 nodes in 7.53 sS393A. Bigger models and frequent swaps lean on high-bandwidth SSDs.

④ KV 缓存外溢④ KV-cache spill

DeepSeek 2025 年 2 月某日 6,080 亿输入 token 中 56.3% 命中磁盘 KV 缓存S23A；NVIDIA 新增以太网连接的 G3.5 上下文闪存层（CMX），宣称 token/s 与能效最高 5 倍S29S30A。On one day in Feb 2025, 56.3% of DeepSeek's 608B input tokens hit its on-disk KV cacheS23A; NVIDIA added an Ethernet-attached G3.5 context-flash tier (CMX), claiming up to 5× tokens/s and power efficiencyS29S30A.

⑤ 向量库与 RAG⑤ Vector databases and RAG

TrendForce：QLC 因成本与容量优势成为向量库与（中国）DeepSeek 式 KV 卸载的主力，QLC 在向量库的渗透率持续上升S270S209B。TrendForce: QLC's cost and capacity make it the workhorse for vector databases and, in China, DeepSeek-style KV offload, and its penetration of vector databases keeps risingS270S209B.

⑥ 替代 HDD 的温数据⑥ Warm data off HDD

美光称 6600 ION（245TB）相对 HDD 机架数减少 82%、能效最高提升 84 倍（厂商口径）S286B；QLC 比近线 HDD 省电约 30%S272B。但目前每 TB 价格仍是 HDD 的 15–19 倍S298B。Micron claims its 245TB 6600 ION needs 82% fewer racks than HDD with up to 84× better energy efficiency (vendor claim)S286B; QLC uses ~30% less power than nearline HDDS272B. But per TB it still costs 15–19× HDD todayS298B.

技术与壁垒：层数、键合与主控Technology and barriers: layers, bonding and controllers

一个容易忽略的细节：2026 年上半年企业级 SSD 的放量主要用的是"老"节点（三星 176/236 层、SK 海力士 176 层 TLC、铠侠 218 层、美光 232 层 QLC），最新的 286–332 层还没有成为企业级主力S268S267D。这意味着即便新层数推迟，企业级 SSD 的供给也未必立刻受限；反过来，新节点放量会在 2027 年带来较大的 bit 增量——这正是 TrendForce 认为 2H27 转松的原因之一S212B。An easily missed detail: in 1H26 enterprise SSD volume shipped mostly on older nodes (Samsung 176/236 layers, SK hynix 176-layer TLC, Kioxia 218, Micron 232-layer QLC); the newest 286–332-layer nodes were not yet dominant in eSSDS268S267D. So a delay at the newest layers does not immediately cap eSSD supply; conversely, new nodes ramping in 2027 add a large bit increment, one reason TrendForce expects easing in 2H27S212B.

TLC、QLC 与 PLCTLC, QLC and PLC

每个单元存 3/4/5 bit。QLC 每 bit 更便宜、更省电，但写入更慢、寿命更短，适合读多写少的向量库、KV 冷层与温数据；三星预计 2H26 的 QLC SSD 出货将比 1H26 翻倍以上S278B。本次调研未找到 2026 年商用 PLC 产品C。Cells hold 3/4/5 bits. QLC is cheaper per bit and uses less power but writes slower and wears faster, suiting read-mostly vector DBs, cold KV and warm data; Samsung expects 2H26 QLC SSD shipments to more than double 1H26S278B. We found no commercial PLC product in 2026C.

主控与固件Controllers and firmware

主控决定 IOPS、延迟与寿命。慧荣 SM8466（PCIe 6.0）在参考设计中，Gen7 内部样品计划 2H27；群联 2Q26 营收同比 +280%S394，并称 2025 年 11 月"每家 NAND 厂都说 2026 年已售罄"，1Tb TLC 颗粒从 4.80 美元涨到 10.70 美元S288S290B。三星 PM1763 用自研 4nm 主控S284A。The controller sets IOPS, latency and endurance. Silicon Motion's SM8466 (PCIe 6.0) is in reference designs, with Gen7 internal samples planned for 2H27; Phison's 2Q26 revenue rose 280% YoYS394, and it said in Nov 2025 that "every NAND manufacturer told us 2026 sold out", with a 1Tb TLC die going from $4.80 to $10.70S288S290B. Samsung's PM1763 uses an in-house 4nm controllerS284A.

供给：克制的资本开支与"转产 DRAM"Supply: restrained capex and conversions to DRAM

与 DRAM 不同，NAND 厂商在这一轮选择了克制：TrendForce 估 2026 年 NAND 资本开支仅 222 亿美元（+5%），DRAM 则 +14%S216B；Omdia 估三星与 SK 海力士 2026 年 NAND 投片分别减少 4.5% 与 10%S280B。三星最后一条 2D NAND 产线改为 1c DRAM，平泽 P4 也转向 DRAMS279B。逆势扩张的是铠侠与闪迪：到 2032 年在日本投资超过 310 亿美元，北上 Fab3 目标 FY2029 投产S281A。中国方面，长江存储 4Q28 前产能约从 20 万片/月增至 40–50 万片/月S338B。Unlike DRAM, NAND makers stayed restrained this cycle: TrendForce puts 2026 NAND capex at just $22.2bn (+5%) versus DRAM's +14%S216B; Omdia estimates Samsung and SK hynix cut 2026 NAND wafer starts 4.5% and 10%S280B. Samsung's last 2D NAND line becomes 1c DRAM, and Pyeongtaek P4 shifts to DRAMS279B. Kioxia and SanDisk are the exception, investing over $31bn in Japan through 2032, with Kitakami Fab3 targeted for FY2029S281A. In China, YMTC lifts capacity from ~200K to 400–500K wafers/month by 4Q28S338B.

KV 缓存能带来多少 NAND 需求：三种估算与一个反例How much NAND demand can the KV cache create? Three estimates and a counterexample

来源Source估算Estimate可信度与口径Reliability and basis
花旗Citi每颗 Vera Rubin GPU 16 TB、每 NVL72 1,152 TB；2026 年 3 万台 → 34.6 EB（全球 NAND 2.8%），2027 年 10 万台 → 115.2 EB（9.3%）16 TB per Vera Rubin GPU, 1,152 TB per NVL72; 30k systems in 2026 → 34.6 EB (2.8% of NAND), 100k in 2027 → 115.2 EB (9.3%)二手转述；两年分母相同（约 1.24 ZB），基数可能是静态的CSecondary relay; both years imply the same ~1.24 ZB denominator, which looks staticCS50S297
闪迪SanDisk2027 年 KV 缓存带来 75–100 EB 增量需求、次年翻倍；到 2030 年 KV 占 AI 数据中心 NAND 负载约 35%；企业数据中心闪存 2030 年 1.2 ZBKV cache adds 75–100 EB in 2027 and doubles the year after; KV ~35% of AI data-center NAND workloads by 2030; enterprise DC flash 1.2 ZB by 20301.2 ZB 为一手；35% 为二手转述C1.2 ZB is primary; the 35% is a secondary relayCS296S294S295
NVIDIA 的机架算术NVIDIA's rack arithmetic一个 BlueField-4 存储机架 = 16 个机箱 × 4 颗 BlueField-4 × 150 TB = 9.6 PB；1,152 颗 GPU × 16 TB = 18.4 PBOne BlueField-4 storage rack = 16 enclosures × 4 BlueField-4 × 150 TB = 9.6 PB; 1,152 GPUs × 16 TB = 18.4 PB媒体按 NVIDIA 架构推算Media arithmetic on NVIDIA's architectureS52
反例：DeepSeek V4.1-FlashCounterexample: DeepSeek V4.1-Flash持久化 KV（"总在 SSD 或主机内存"）降到 V4-Flash 的约 1/8；某交易员估 AI 闪存需求因此少约 31%Persistent KV ("always on SSD or in host memory") falls to ~1/8 of V4-Flash; one trader estimated ~31% less AI flash demand1/8 为公司论文口径；31% 未经核实C1/8 is the company's paper; the 31% is unverifiedCS95S401

这些估算的分歧，本质是对"每 token KV 大小 × 保留时长 × 会话数"三个变量的不同假设。§11 的交互模型 M3 让你自己调这三个变量：在 GQA 模型、长保留期的假设下，每 GPU 需要数 TB 闪存；换成 V4.1 式压缩，同样的业务只要几十 GB。结论是：KV 闪存需求是真实的，但它对模型架构极其敏感，是整个存储需求里"方差最大"的一块。These estimates differ mainly in their assumptions about three variables: KV bytes per token × retention time × number of sessions. Model M3 in §11 lets you set them: with a GQA model and long retention, each GPU needs several TB of flash; with V4.1-style compression the same workload needs tens of GB. KV flash demand is real, but extremely sensitive to model architecture: it is the highest-variance piece of all memory demand.

HBF：把闪存放进封装的尝试HBF: putting flash inside the package

高带宽闪存（High Bandwidth Flash）借用 HBM 的思路：把 8 或 16 层 NAND 裸片用 TSV 叠起来，通过 UCIe 接口靠近计算芯片。2026-08-03/04 发布的首版 OCP 规范定义：最高 512 GB，三个带宽档约 0.4、1.5、3.0 TB/sS135S371A。闪迪一代产品为 16 层（每颗 256Gb）512 GB、读 1.6 TB/s，与 HBM4 同尺寸；二代最高 1 TB、超过 2 TB/s；三代 1.5 TB、3.2 TB/sS136A。闪迪称 4 颗配 HBF 的 GPU 可产出与 8 颗纯 HBM GPU 相同的 tokenS134B。High Bandwidth Flash borrows HBM's idea: stack 8 or 16 NAND dies with TSVs and place them near compute over UCIe. The first OCP spec (Aug 3–4, 2026) allows up to 512 GB with three bandwidth grades of ~0.4, 1.5 and 3.0 TB/sS135S371A. SanDisk's gen 1 is 16 dies (256Gb each) for 512 GB at 1.6 TB/s read in HBM4's footprint; gen 2 reaches up to 1 TB at >2 TB/s, gen 3 1.5 TB at 3.2 TB/sS136A. SanDisk says 4 GPUs with HBF match the token output of 8 HBM-only GPUsS134B.

时间Date事件Event
2025-08-06闪迪与 SK 海力士签署 MoU：容量为 HBM 的 8–16 倍、成本相近；原计划 2H26 送样S133SanDisk–SK hynix MoU: 8–16× HBM capacity at similar cost; samples first planned for 2H26S133
2026-02-25标准化联盟启动，进入 OCP 工作组Standardization consortium launched under OCP
2026-08-03/04首版 OCP 规范：闪迪、SK 海力士、谷歌、TenstorrentS135S371First OCP spec: SanDisk, SK hynix, Google, TenstorrentS135S371
2026-08-13/14闪迪称首款 HBF 已流片，2027 送样、2028 量产；NVIDIA 尚未宣布采用S134S294SanDisk: first HBF taped out, samples 2027, volume 2028; NVIDIA has announced no adoptionS134S294

▲为什么可能成Why it could work

  1. 推理权重"读多写少"，正好避开 NAND 写慢、寿命短的弱点（"训练时权重一直在变，推理时不动"）S138B。Inference weights are read-mostly, sidestepping NAND's slow writes and wear ("during inference, you leave them alone")S138B.
  2. 以接近 HBM 的价格提供 8–16 倍容量，可放下万亿参数 MoE 的全部专家S133A。8–16× capacity at a near-HBM price could hold every expert of a trillion-parameter MoES133A.
  3. 谷歌与 Tenstorrent 已加入规范，存在 ASIC 首发路径S135A。Google and Tenstorrent joined the spec, offering an ASIC-first pathS135A.

▼为什么可能不成Why it may not

  1. 写入耐久度"在几千次量级"，写速度是闪存最弱的环节；延迟不太可能接近 HBM4S138B。Endurance is "in the level of thousands" of cycles and writes are flash's weak spot; latency is unlikely to approach HBM4S138B.
  2. 进度已比 2025 年计划推迟约一年；TechRadar 认为大规模量产要"接近 2030 年"S134S137B。It is already about a year behind the 2025 plan; TechRadar thinks volume is justified only "closer to 2030"S134S137B.
  3. NVIDIA、美光、铠侠不在规范成员中（三星是否参与各源说法不一）S137C。NVIDIA, Micron and Kioxia are not named members (sources differ on Samsung)S137C.

NAND 的演进方向Where NAND is heading

  • 层数：400+ → 500 层（三星 V11），三星目标 2030 年 1,000 层，铁电（铪基）NAND 被视为关键材料S275S402B。Layers: 400+ → 500 (Samsung V11); Samsung targets 1,000 layers by 2030, with ferroelectric (hafnia) NAND seen as keyS275S402B.
  • 接口与 IOPS：PCIe 6.0 普及、Gen7 2H27 起送样；GPU 直连的 5,000 万–1 亿 IOPS 盘面向小块 KV 与嵌入表S288B。Interfaces and IOPS: PCIe 6.0 spreads and Gen7 samples from 2H27; GPU-direct 50–100M IOPS drives target small KV blocks and embedding tablesS288B.
  • 容量：512 TB → 1 PB 级 QLC，进一步替代温数据 HDDS287B。Capacity: 512 TB → PB-class QLC, displacing more warm-data HDDS287B.
  • 系统："上下文内存"存储系统（VAST、WEKA、DDN、Everpure、NetApp、华为 OceanStor M900，后者 KV 底层最高 64 PB、约 60 µs）S304S305S306S308S309S307AB。Systems: "context memory" storage (VAST, WEKA, DDN, Everpure, NetApp, Huawei's OceanStor M900 with up to 64 PB of KV base tier at ~60 µs)S304S305S306S308S309S307AB.
判断Judgment

NAND 的结构性变化是"企业级化"：数据中心在 2026 年约占 NAND 营收的一半（2025 年约 30%）S293A。KV 缓存与 QLC 替代 HDD 让 NAND 第一次成为 AI 推理的性能部件。但 NAND 的供给弹性比 DRAM 大（工艺迁移即可增 bit），2027 年新节点放量与 KV 压缩是两大下行风险，所以 TrendForce 的"2H27 转松"与厂商的"紧到 2027 年以后"之争，是未来一年最值得跟踪的分歧之一。NAND's structural change is enterprise-ization: data centers are about half of NAND revenue in 2026, up from ~30% in 2025S293A. The KV cache and QLC displacing HDD make NAND an inference-performance component for the first time. But NAND supply is more elastic than DRAM (migration alone adds bits), and new nodes in 2027 plus KV compression are the two main downside risks. The split between TrendForce ("easing in 2H27") and suppliers ("tight beyond 2027") is one of the most important disagreements to track over the next year.

HDD 磁带Tape

07

HDD 与磁带：AI 数据湖的地基HDD and tape: the foundation of AI data lakes

负责什么Role原始爬取与数据湖、训练数据集冷层、checkpoint 归档、日志与 Agent 产物、视频与多模态素材Raw crawl and data lakes, cold tier of training sets, checkpoint archives, logs and agent artifacts, video and multimodal assets
关键特性Traits单盘 24–44 TB；随机访问 ~4–10 ms；顺序吞吐约 0.25–0.3 GB/s；单位成本最低的在线介质24–44 TB per drive; ~4–10 ms random access; ~0.25–0.3 GB/s sequential; the cheapest online medium

供需Supply交期 >52 周；希捷近线大部分已分配到 CY2028；西数 CY2027 大部分在长约下>52-week lead times; Seagate nearline mostly allocated into CY2028; WD's CY2027 mostly under LTAsS272S317S318

主要玩家Players希捷、西部数据、东芝；部件：TDK（磁头）、Resonac（介质）、HOYA（玻璃基板）Seagate, Western Digital, Toshiba; parts: TDK (heads), Resonac (media), HOYA (glass substrates)

AI 训练要先"攒数据"：Common Crawl 每月抓取约 21 亿页、约 360 TiB 原始数据，一年约 4.3 PiBS21AD；经过过滤与去重后，FineWeb 这样 18.5 万亿 token 的数据集只有约 50 TBS20A。也就是说，原始数据是 PB 级，最终训练用的 token 只有数十 TB——前者躺在 HDD 与对象存储里。西数管理层称超大规模数据中心约 80% 的数据存放在 HDD 上，并把 Agent 称为"容量型存储需求的结构性、阶跃式驱动力"，因为"Agent 在工作流的每一步都在产生数据"S319S324B。Training starts with collecting data: Common Crawl fetches ~2.1bn pages and ~360 TiB of raw data a month, ~4.3 PiB a yearS21AD; after filtering and deduplication, an 18.5-trillion-token dataset like FineWeb is only ~50 TBS20A. Raw data is petabytes; the final training tokens are tens of terabytes, and the former sits on HDD and object storage. WD's management says ~80% of hyperscale data-center data lives on HDD and calls agentic AI "a structural and step function driver of capacity-oriented storage demand", because "agents generate data at every step of a workflow"S319S324B.

容量怎么长出来：台数 × 碟片数 × 每碟容量How capacity grows: units × platters × TB per platter

行业体量Industry size

2025 年全球 HDD 出货 1.243 亿台（+0.4%）、1,621 EB（+22%）、营收 233 亿美元（+18%），其中近线 8,080 万台；4Q25 平均每台 14.0 TBS314B。台数多年持平，增长全部来自单盘容量。Coughlin 预计 2026 年营收超过 300 亿美元，为约 2014 年以来最高S315B。In 2025 the industry shipped 124.3M drives (+0.4%), 1,621 EB (+22%) and $23.3bn of revenue (+18%), including 80.8M nearline units; the 4Q25 average was 14.0 TB per driveS314B. Units have been flat for years; all growth comes from capacity per drive. Coughlin expects 2026 revenue above $30bn, the highest since about 2014S315B.

部件"卡脖子"Component chokepoints

独立磁头供应商只有 TDK，独立介质供应商只有 Resonac，玻璃基板毛坯主要来自 HOYA。Resonac 已把介质产能从 1.6 亿片/年扩到 2.1 亿片，再追加新加坡投资；TDK、HOYA 也在扩产S320S321AB。这些部件决定了没有自制磁头和介质的厂商（如东芝）能扩多快。TDK is the only merchant head maker, Resonac the only merchant media maker, and HOYA the main source of glass substrate blanks. Resonac is lifting media capacity from 160M to 210M disks a year and adding Singapore investment; TDK and HOYA are expanding tooS320S321AB. These parts set how fast a maker without in-house heads and media, such as Toshiba, can grow.

供需：售罄、长约与东芝的"翻倍"Supply and demand: sold out, LTAs and Toshiba's doubling

近线 HDD 的交期在 2025 年 9 月就已超过 52 周S272B；希捷称近线 EB 的"绝大部分已分配至 CY2028"，按单生产合同覆盖整个 CY2027S317A；西数称 CY2027 的"绝大部分"已在长约之下S318A。2026-10-02，日经报道东芝将投资约 600 亿日元，在 FY2027 内把 AI 数据中心用 HDD 产能（存储容量口径）较 FY2025 翻倍，希捷与西数当日分别收跌 10.21% 与 10.22%S313S312BA。上一份研报的结论是：这是一次"久期冲击"，而非"盈利冲击"——近端产能已被锁定，真正的风险在 2028–2031 年多股供给同时落地。Nearline HDD lead times already exceeded 52 weeks in Sep 2025S272B. Seagate says the "vast majority" of its nearline exabytes are allocated into CY2028, with build-to-order contracts covering all of CY2027S317A; WD has the "vast majority" of CY2027 under LTAsS318A. On Oct 2, 2026, Nikkei reported Toshiba will invest ~¥60bn to double its AI data-center HDD capacity (in exabytes) by FY2027 versus FY2025; Seagate and WD closed down 10.21% and 10.22%S313S312BA. Our earlier report concluded it was a duration shock, not an earnings shock: near-term capacity is locked, and the real risk is several supply additions landing together in 2028–2031.

HDD 与 QLC 的边界Where HDD meets QLC

同为 30 TB，HDD 街价约 1,216 美元（41 美元/TB），TLC SSD 约 22,600 美元（753 美元/TB），倍数 18.6；QLC 约 14.9 倍S298BD。HDD 在每 TB 成本上仍有一个数量级的优势，所以冷数据、海量顺序数据仍会留在 HDD；QLC 主要吃掉"对延迟敏感的温数据"。At 30 TB, an HDD costs ~$1,216 street ($41/TB) and a TLC SSD ~$22,600 ($753/TB), an 18.6× multiple; QLC is ~14.9×S298BD. HDD keeps an order-of-magnitude cost edge per TB, so cold and bulk sequential data stays on disk; QLC mainly takes latency-sensitive warm data.

AI 对 HDD 的真实拉动AI's real pull on HDD

AI 不直接"读"HDD 做计算，它拉动的是数据的产生与留存：多模态训练素材、合成数据、模型版本、评测记录、推理日志与 Agent 产物。WD 单季出货 231 EBS319B。这部分需求与 token 量和数据保留政策相关，而与 KV 压缩基本无关，因此是存储链里受算法效率冲击最小的一环。AI does not compute directly off HDD; it drives data creation and retention: multimodal training material, synthetic data, model versions, evaluation records, inference logs and agent artifacts. WD shipped 231 EB in one quarterS319B. This demand tracks token volume and retention policy and is largely unaffected by KV compression, making it the link least exposed to algorithmic efficiency.

磁带：被低估的反弹Tape: an underrated rebound

B&F extrapolates ~252 EB for 2026 from first-quarter growth (its own extrapolation, not LTO data)S300B. DNA storage is at an early commercial stage: Biomemory plans its first end-to-end product before end-2026 and a rack appliance in 2030–31, with negligible capacity this decadeS145B.

新兴存储Emerging memory 近存 / 存内计算Near- / in-memory compute

08

新兴存储与近存计算：哪些会改变格局，哪些只是"增加供给"Emerging memory and near-memory compute: what changes the game and what only adds supply

每一轮存储紧缺都会让"替代技术"重新受到关注。判断它们的关键问题只有两个：它替代的是哪一层的需求？它什么时候能以数据中心规模供货？下表把主要候选按这两个问题排开。Every memory shortage revives interest in alternatives. Two questions decide each one: which tier's demand does it replace, and when can it ship at data-center scale? The table sorts the main candidates by those questions.

数据表Data table
依据：HBM4ES166、定制 HBM4ES183、HBFS134、LPDDR6-PIMS128、MRDIMM Gen3S242、PCIe 7.0/1 亿 IOPSS288、4F² 与 High-NAS222S223、混合键合 HBM5S194S184、全 3D DRAMS130、铠侠北上 Fab3S281、Celestial 收入S125、SOT-MRAMS139、DNA 机架S145。Basis: HBM4ES166, custom HBM4ES183, HBFS134, LPDDR6-PIMS128, MRDIMM Gen3S242, PCIe 7.0 / 100M IOPSS288, 4F² and High-NAS222S223, hybrid-bonded HBM5S194S184, full 3D DRAMS130, Kioxia Kitakami Fab3S281, Celestial revenueS125, SOT-MRAMS139, DNA racksS145.
判断Judgment

2028 年之前，没有任何新型介质能在数据中心规模上替代 HBM 或 DRAM。真正会在 2027–2030 年改变格局的是"结构性"技术：定制 HBM 基底、混合键合、4F² 与 3D DRAM、HBF。前三者让 DRAM 更强、更多（增加供给与价值），HBF 则是唯一可能把部分 HBM 容量需求转移到 NAND 的路线——但它要到 2028 年才量产，且 NVIDIA 尚未表态。Before 2028 no new medium can replace HBM or DRAM at data-center scale. What can change the game in 2027–2030 are structural technologies: custom HBM base dies, hybrid bonding, 4F² and 3D DRAM, and HBF. The first three make DRAM stronger and more plentiful (more supply and value); HBF is the only route that could move some HBM capacity demand to NAND, and it does not reach volume until 2028, with NVIDIA yet to commit.

AI 工作流 · 训练Workloads · Training

09

训练全流程：从数据湖到强化学习，每一步卡在哪Training, step by step: from data lake to RL, where each step is bound

一次前沿模型训练可以拆成八步。前三步是"数据工程"，主要压在 HDD、CPU 内存和 SSD 上；第四、五步是真正的"算"，压在 HBM 和互联上；第六步是容错，压在突发写带宽与内存冗余上；最后两步（强化学习 rollout 与合成数据）本质上是推理，压在 HBM 带宽和 KV 容量上。下图每一步的色标是该步最依赖的存储层。A frontier training run splits into eight steps. The first three are data engineering, leaning on HDD, CPU memory and SSD. Steps four and five are the real compute, leaning on HBM and interconnect. Step six is fault tolerance, leaning on burst write bandwidth and in-memory redundancy. The last two (RL rollouts and synthetic data) are inference at heart, leaning on HBM bandwidth and KV capacity. Each step's colour bar marks the tier it leans on most.

T1数据采集与数据湖Crawl and data lakePB 级原始网页、代码、多模态素材PB-scale raw web, code, multimodal assetsHDDSSD
T2清洗、去重、分词Filter, dedupe, tokenizeCPU 批处理，数十 TB tokenCPU batch jobs; tens of TB of tokensDRAMSSD
T3数据加载Data loading文本很轻；视频与多模态很重Light for text; heavy for video and multimodalSSDDRAM
T4前向与反向Forward and backward模型状态与激活必须装进 HBMModel states and activations must fit in HBMHBMSRAM
T5梯度同步与并行Gradient sync, parallelism张量/专家并行留在 scale-up 域Tensor/expert parallel stays in the scale-up domain互联InterconnectHBM
T6Checkpoint 与恢复Checkpoint and recoveryTB 级同步写；十万卡下每十几分钟一次故障TB-scale synchronous writes; a failure every ~14 min at 131K GPUsDRAMNVMe
T7强化学习 rolloutRL rollouts占每轮迭代 63–87% 的时间，本质是推理63–87% of each iteration; inference at heartHBMDRAM
T8合成数据与蒸馏Synthetic data, distillation大规模推理生成训练数据Large-scale inference to make training dataHBMHDD

T1–T3 数据工程：原始数据 PB 级，喂进 GPU 的只有每秒十几 MBT1–T3 data engineering: petabytes of raw data, a dozen MB/s into the GPUs

数据集规模：Llama 3 约 15 万亿 token（405B 模型训练了 15.6 万亿），Llama 4 超过 30 万亿，Qwen3 约 36 万亿，DeepSeek-V3 14.8 万亿，Kimi K2 15.5 万亿S1S406S407S4S6A。Llama 3 的 12.8 万词表需要 32 位 token ID，15.6 万亿 token 约 62 TBD。真正的存储大头在上游：原始爬取与清洗中间产物以 PB 计，躺在 HDD 与对象存储上；Nemotron-CC 甚至用分类器与合成改写来"回收"本会被丢弃的约 90% 数据S405A。Dataset sizes: Llama 3 ~15T tokens (15.6T for the 405B model), Llama 4 over 30T, Qwen3 ~36T, DeepSeek-V3 14.8T, Kimi K2 15.5TS1S406S407S4S6A. Llama 3's 128K vocabulary needs 32-bit token IDs, so 15.6T tokens take ~62 TBD. The bulk sits upstream: raw crawl and intermediate products run to petabytes on HDD and object storage; Nemotron-CC even uses classifiers and synthetic rephrasing to recover the ~90% of data that would otherwise be droppedS405A.

算一笔账：数据加载并不是瓶颈The arithmetic: data loading is not the bottleneck

405B × 6 FLOP/token ≈ 2.43×10¹² FLOP/token · 400 TFLOPS/GPU ÷ 2.43×10¹² ≈ 165 token/s/GPU · ×16,384 GPU ≈ 2.7M token/s · ×4 B ≈ 11 MB/s

Llama 3 405B 每颗 GPU 约 380–430 TFLOPSS1A，整个 1.6 万卡集群每秒只需约 270 万 token、约 11 MB 文本D。NVIDIA 的 SuperPOD 存储指南也说 NLP 数据集"通常能放进本地缓存"，深度学习训练的关键 I/O 是"反复重读"，内存是第一层缓存S43A。例外是视频与多模态：谷歌称 TPU 8t 的存储访问对多模态数据集快 10 倍S66A。Llama 3 405B ran at ~380–430 TFLOPS per GPUS1A, so the whole 16K-GPU cluster consumed only ~2.7M tokens and ~11 MB of text per secondD. NVIDIA's SuperPOD storage guide notes that NLP datasets "generally fit within local cache", that the key I/O in DL training is re-reads, and that RAM is the first cache layerS43A. The exception is video and multimodal: Google pitches TPU 8t storage access as 10× faster for multimodal datasetsS66A.

来源：S43S44S37S42。NVIDIA 建议 LLM 训练的写带宽至少为读的一半——主要是为了 checkpoint。Sources: S43S44S37S42. NVIDIA advises LLM write bandwidth of at least half the read bandwidth, mainly for checkpoints.

T4 前向与反向：真正的 HBM 之战T4 forward and backward: the real fight for HBM

训练时 HBM 里要放四样东西：权重、梯度、优化器状态和激活值。混合精度 Adam 的"模型状态"是 2（FP16 权重）+ 2（FP16 梯度）+ 12（FP32 主权重、一阶与二阶矩）= 16 字节/参数S2A；DeepSeek 与 Kimi 保留 FP32 梯度时为 18 字节S4S6D；DeepSeek-V3 的 FP8 方案（BF16 矩、FP8 缓存激活、部分算子重算）约 12–14 字节S4D。During training HBM holds four things: weights, gradients, optimizer states and activations. Mixed-precision Adam's model state is 2 (FP16 weights) + 2 (FP16 gradients) + 12 (FP32 master weights plus first and second moments) = 16 bytes per parameterS2A; with FP32 gradients, as DeepSeek and Kimi keep, it is 18S4S6D; DeepSeek-V3's FP8 recipe (BF16 moments, FP8-cached activations, some recomputation) is ~12–14S4D.

模型规模Model size模型状态（16 B/参数）Model state (16 B/param)至少需要多少颗 80GB GPUMin 80GB GPUs至少需要多少颗 288GB GPUMin 288GB GPUs可重启 checkpoint（14 B/参数）Restartable checkpoint (14 B/param)
70B1.12 TB1440.98 TB
405B6.48 TB81235.67 TB
1T16 TB2005614 TB
2T32 TB40011228 TB

仅模型状态，未含激活与缓冲D。Kimi K2（1T 参数）实际用 256 颗 GPU 的模型并行组承载约 6 TB 状态，每卡约 30 GB，并把剩余激活卸载到 CPU 内存，每节点 2 TB 内存S6A。Model state only, excluding activations and buffersD. Kimi K2 (1T parameters) actually spread ~6 TB of state over a 256-GPU model-parallel group, ~30 GB per GPU, and offloaded remaining activations to CPU memory with 2 TB per nodeS6A.

激活值往往比模型状态更大。按 Korthikanti 等人的公式，每层激活约 s·b·h·(34 + 5as/h) 字节；用 FlashAttention 免存注意力分数后约为 34·s·b·hS3A。以 405B（h=16,384）跑一条 8K 序列为例，每层约 4.6 GB、126 层约 575 GB——远超任何单卡D。解决办法都是"拿别的资源换 HBM"：Activations are often larger than model state. By Korthikanti et al., each layer's activations take ~s·b·h·(34 + 5as/h) bytes, or ~34·s·b·h once FlashAttention avoids storing attention scoresS3A. For 405B (h = 16,384) on one 8K sequence that is ~4.6 GB per layer and ~575 GB over 126 layers, far beyond any single GPUD. Every fix trades another resource for HBM:

  • 切分（拿通信换容量）：ZeRO/FSDP 把每卡模型状态从 16Ψ 降到 16Ψ/N（7.5B 模型 64 路数据并行：120 GB → 1.9 GB）S2A；Llama 3 用张量、上下文、流水线、数据四维并行，让每卡"参数、优化器状态、梯度和激活都装进 HBM"S1A。代价是通信量上升，所以 HBM 越大，需要的切分与通信越少。Sharding (communication for capacity): ZeRO/FSDP cuts per-device model state from 16Ψ to 16Ψ/N (a 7.5B model on 64-way data parallel: 120 GB → 1.9 GB)S2A; Llama 3 used 4D parallelism (tensor, context, pipeline, data) so each GPU's "parameters, optimizer states, gradients, and activations fit in its HBM"S1A. The cost is more communication, so more HBM means less sharding and less traffic.
  • 重算（拿算力换容量）：选择性重算让激活内存减少 5 倍、重算开销降低 90% 以上；全量重算开销约 39%，选择性约 4%；530B 模型在 2,240 颗 A100 上的 MFU 因此从 42.1% 提到 54.2%S3A。Recomputation (compute for capacity): selective recomputation cuts activation memory 5× and recompute overhead by over 90%; full recompute costs ~39% versus ~4% for selective; a 530B model on 2,240 A100s rose from 42.1% to 54.2% MFUS3A.
  • 卸载（拿 CPU 内存换 HBM）：DeepSeek 把 EMA 放在 CPU 内存，Kimi K2 把剩余激活卸载到 CPUS4S6A——训练也开始依赖 CPU 侧 DRAM。Offload (CPU memory for HBM): DeepSeek keeps its EMA in CPU memory and Kimi K2 offloads remaining activations to the CPUS4S6A, so training also leans on CPU-side DRAM.
  • 降精度（拿数值空间换容量与带宽）：DeepSeek-V3 首次在 671B 规模验证 FP8 训练，Nemotron 3 用 NVFP4 训练S4S105A。Lower precision (numeric range for capacity and bandwidth): DeepSeek-V3 was first to validate FP8 training at 671B scale, and Nemotron 3 trained in NVFP4S4S105A.

T5 并行与互联：HBM 容量决定通信量T5 parallelism and interconnect: HBM capacity sets the traffic

张量并行与专家并行（含 MoE 的 all-to-all）的通信必须留在 scale-up 域内。每颗 GPU 的 scale-up 带宽：H100 900 GB/s，B200/B300 1.8 TB/s，Rubin 3.6 TB/s（产品页写 3 TB/s）S34S26S27AC。DeepSeek-V3 在 2,048 颗 H800 上训练，因为 H800 的 NVLink 只有 160 GB/s、InfiniBand 50 GB/s，干脆"不用昂贵的张量并行"，改用 16 路流水线、64 路专家并行与 ZeRO-1，并在论文里呼吁硬件厂商统一 scale-up 与 scale-out 网络S4A。这说明：互联不足时，更大的 HBM 可以减少切分；HBM 不足时，必须用更强的互联去切分。两者是同一个约束的两面。Tensor- and expert-parallel traffic (including MoE all-to-all) must stay inside the scale-up domain. Scale-up bandwidth per GPU: H100 900 GB/s, B200/B300 1.8 TB/s, Rubin 3.6 TB/s (3 TB/s on the product page)S34S26S27AC. DeepSeek-V3 trained on 2,048 H800s, whose NVLink is only 160 GB/s and InfiniBand 50 GB/s, and so skipped "costly tensor parallelism" for 16-way pipeline, 64-way expert parallelism and ZeRO-1, and asked vendors to unify scale-up and scale-out networksS4A. When interconnect is short, bigger HBM reduces sharding; when HBM is short, stronger interconnect must do the sharding. They are two faces of one constraint.

T6 Checkpoint 与故障：规模越大，存储越重要T6 checkpoints and failures: the bigger the run, the more storage matters

Llama 3：54 天、419 次非计划中断的根因Llama 3: root causes of 419 unplanned interruptions in 54 days
红色为存储相关（HBM3、GPU SRAM、系统内存），合计约 22%Red marks memory-related causes (HBM3, GPU SRAM, system memory), ~22% together

数据表Data table
S7S1；MTTF ≈ 29,500 GPU·小时 ÷ GPU 数D。S7S1; MTTF ≈ 29,500 GPU-hours ÷ GPU countD.

Meta 推算：1.6 万卡集群平均 1.8 小时故障一次，13.1 万卡只有约 14 分钟；一个 1.6 万卡、每 60 分钟做一次 checkpoint 的任务，有效训练时间只有 70%S7A。1T 参数的可重启 checkpoint 约 14 TB：按 Meta Tectonic 的 2 TB/s 写约 7 秒，按 B200 SuperPOD 增强档 4 个 SU 的 250 GB/s 写约 56 秒S1S44D。在 14 分钟一次故障的环境里，"写 checkpoint + 重启"必须压到一分钟量级，于是业界转向：Meta projects a 16K-GPU cluster fails every 1.8 hours and a 131K-GPU cluster every ~14 minutes; a 16K-GPU job checkpointing hourly would keep only 70% effective training timeS7A. A restartable 1T checkpoint is ~14 TB: ~7 s at Meta Tectonic's 2 TB/s, ~56 s at 250 GB/s (four SUs of B200 SuperPOD Enhanced)S1S44D. With a failure every 14 minutes, write-plus-restart must shrink to about a minute, so the industry has moved to:

  • 内存内冗余：Gemini 用内存中的冗余副本取代从持久存储恢复，最大任务 goodput 从 85% 升到 97%；静默数据损坏预计"每一两周一次"S8A。谷歌 TPU 8t 的目标是 goodput 超过 97%S66A。In-memory redundancy: Gemini replaced restores from persistent storage with redundant in-memory replicas, lifting goodput on its largest job from 85% to 97%; it expected silent data corruption "every week or two"S8A. Google targets more than 97% goodput on TPU 8tS66A.
  • 异步与分片 checkpoint：字节跳动 ByteCheckpoint 平均把 checkpoint 停顿减少 54.2 倍S19A。Asynchronous, sharded checkpoints: ByteDance's ByteCheckpoint cuts checkpoint stalls by 54.2× on averageS19A.
  • 更快的本地层：本地 NVMe 先接住突发写，再异步下沉到并行文件系统与 HDD 归档。这正是训练对 CPU 内存与 NVMe 的"隐性需求"。Faster local tiers: local NVMe absorbs the burst, then drains asynchronously to parallel file systems and HDD archives. This is training's hidden demand for CPU memory and NVMe.

T7–T8 强化学习与合成数据：训练里的"推理"T7–T8 RL and synthetic data: inference inside training

工作负载（Seer 论文）Workload (Seer paper)rollout训练Training权重同步Weight sync平均输出长度Mean output
Moonlight84%14%2%22,386 token22,386 tokens
Qwen2-VL-72B63%31%6%7,615 token7,615 tokens
Kimi-K287%10%3%38,959 token（最长 96K）38,959 tokens (up to 96K)

强化学习后训练的大部分时间在"生成"：Seer 统计 rollout 约占迭代时间的 80%，一条推理请求的 KV 缓存"可能从几百 MB 开始，膨胀到数十 GB"，导致大量请求被抢占S10A；RollPacker 发现最长响应是中位数的 25–32 倍（长尾）S11A；Sparse-RL 把不断增长的 KV 缓存称为限制 rollout batch 的"内存墙"S404A。合成数据同理：Nemotron-4 340B 的对齐数据 98% 以上是合成的，Kimi K2 用了 2 万多个合成工具与 3,000 多个真实 MCP 工具来生成 Agent 训练数据S12S6A。所以，越来越多的"训练算力"其实是推理算力，瓶颈也从 HBM 容量转向 HBM 带宽与 KV 容量——这把训练与推理的存储需求连在了一起。Most RL post-training time goes to generation: Seer finds rollouts take ~80% of iteration time, and a reasoning request's KV cache "may begin with only a few hundred megabytes… but expand to tens of gigabytes", causing heavy preemptionS10A; RollPacker finds the longest response is 25–32× the median (a long tail)S11A; Sparse-RL calls the growing KV cache a "memory wall" capping rollout batch sizeS404A. Synthetic data is the same: over 98% of Nemotron-4 340B's alignment data was synthetic, and Kimi K2 used 20,000+ synthetic tools plus 3,000+ real MCP tools to create agentic training dataS12S6A. More and more "training compute" is really inference compute, and the bottleneck shifts from HBM capacity to HBM bandwidth and KV capacity, tying training's memory needs to inference's.

交互模型 M2Interactive model M2
训练内存与容错计算器：装得下吗？多久存一次？有效训练时间多少？Training memory and fault-tolerance calculator: does it fit, how often to checkpoint, how much goodput?
模型状态按 ZeRO 公式，激活按 Korthikanti 公式（FlashAttention 口径），goodput 按 Young/Daly 一阶近似。用于理解量级，不替代实际框架的内存分析。Model state per the ZeRO formulas, activations per Korthikanti (FlashAttention basis), goodput per the Young/Daly first-order approximation. For orders of magnitude, not a substitute for a framework's memory profiler.
预设Presets
稠密模型为 0；DeepSeek/Kimi 约 0.95–0.970 for dense; ~0.95–0.97 for DeepSeek/Kimi
Meta Tectonic 持续 2 TB/s；内存内 checkpoint 可视作更高Meta Tectonic sustains 2 TB/s; in-memory checkpoints act as higher
Meta 外推约 2.95 万；Llama 3 实测约 5 万Meta's projection ~29.5K; Llama 3 observed ~50K

公式：每卡参数 = 稠密部分 ÷ (TP·PP) + 专家部分 ÷ (TP·PP·EP)；ZeRO-1 只切优化器、ZeRO-2 再切梯度、ZeRO-3 全切（按 DP 切分）；激活 = 系数 × s·b·h ÷ (TP·CP) × L（1F1B 下首个流水级同时持有约 PP 个微批，每个微批 L/PP 层）；另加 10% 缓冲。checkpoint = 参数 × 14（FP32 Adam）或 10（BF16 矩）字节；最优间隔 τ* = √(2δM)，goodput ≈ 1 − δ/τ* − (τ*/2 + R)/M。Formulas: per-GPU params = dense ÷ (TP·PP) + experts ÷ (TP·PP·EP); ZeRO-1 shards optimizer, ZeRO-2 also gradients, ZeRO-3 everything (over DP); activations = coefficient × s·b·h ÷ (TP·CP) × L (under 1F1B the first stage holds ~PP micro-batches of L/PP layers each); plus 10% buffers. Checkpoint = params × 14 (FP32 Adam) or 10 (BF16 moments) bytes; optimal interval τ* = √(2δM), goodput ≈ 1 − δ/τ* − (τ*/2 + R)/M.D

AI 工作流 · 推理Workloads · Inference

10

推理全流程：prefill 拼算力，decode 拼带宽，多轮对话拼容量Inference, step by step: prefill is bound by compute, decode by bandwidth, multi-turn by capacity

一次推理请求的生命周期可以拆成七步。关键在于理解两个阶段的本质差异：prefill 一次性处理整段输入，是矩阵乘矩阵，算力受限；decode 每次只生成一个 token，却要把全部权重和这条请求的全部上下文（KV 缓存）读一遍，是矩阵乘向量，带宽受限S38A。NVIDIA 的原话是"上下文阶段受算力约束……生成阶段受内存带宽约束"S33A；Meta 也说 decode 阶段的主要瓶颈是 HBM 带宽S70B。An inference request's life splits into seven steps. The key is the different nature of two phases. Prefill processes the whole input at once as matrix-matrix math and is compute-bound; decode emits one token at a time yet re-reads all the weights and this request's whole context (the KV cache), as matrix-vector math, so it is bandwidth-boundS38A. NVIDIA puts it as "the context phase is compute-bound… the generation phase is memory bandwidth-bound"S33A; Meta likewise calls HBM bandwidth the main decode bottleneckS70B.

I1加载权重Load weightsMoE 权重 0.7–1 TB，冷启动从 SSD 读入MoE weights 0.7–1 TB, cold-started from SSDHBMSSD
I2路由与前缀缓存查找Route, look up prefix cache命中则跳过 prefillA hit skips prefillDRAMCMX
I3Prefill算力受限；长上下文注意力是平方增长Compute-bound; long-context attention grows quadratically算力ComputeHBM
I4KV 传输KV transfer分离式部署中从 prefill 节点传到 decode 节点From prefill to decode nodes in disaggregated servingRDMA
I5Decode每个 token 重读权重与全部 KVEach token re-reads weights and the whole KVHBMSRAM
I6多轮之间保留 KVKeep KV between turnsHBM → CPU 内存 → NVMe → 机架闪存 → 共享存储HBM → CPU memory → NVMe → rack flash → shared storageDRAMSSD
I7采样、工具与编排Sampling, tools, orchestrationCPU 与 CPU 内存CPU and CPU memoryDRAM

算术强度：为什么 decode 总是"饿着"Arithmetic intensity: why decode is always starved

一颗 GPU 每从 HBM 读 1 字节，能做多少次运算才能"不闲着"？这个比值叫平衡点。decode 时，权重部分的算术强度约等于 batch 大小（BF16 下每字节约 B 次运算，FP8 下 2B、FP4 下 4B）；KV 读取则不能在 batch 之间共享，GQA 下每字节只有约 g 次运算（g 为每个 KV 头服务的查询头数，Llama-70B 为 8）D。下表的平衡点动辄数百到两千以上，意味着 decode 需要几百的 batch 才能接近算力上限，而长上下文会把它推得更远。For each byte a GPU reads from HBM, how many operations must it do to stay busy? That ratio is the balance point. In decode the weight part's intensity is roughly the batch size (about B operations per byte at BF16, 2B at FP8, 4B at FP4); KV reads cannot be shared across the batch, so under GQA they offer only ~g operations per byte (g = query heads per KV head, 8 for Llama-70B)D. Balance points in the table run from hundreds to over two thousand, so decode needs batches in the hundreds to approach peak compute, and long contexts push it further away.

芯片Chip精度Precision稠密算力Dense compute存储带宽Memory bandwidth平衡点（FLOP/字节）Balance point (FLOP/byte)
H100BF16 / FP8989 TF / 1.98 PF3.35 TB/s295 / 591
H200BF16 / FP8989 TF / 1.98 PF4.8 TB/s206 / 412
B200FP8 / NVFP45 / 10 PF8 TB/s625 / 1,250
B300NVFP415 PF8 TB/s1,875

由厂商规格推算S34S26S27S63S65S31D。SRAM 派芯片的平衡点只有个位数，所以它们在小 batch、高交互的 decode 上有结构优势。Derived from vendor specsS34S26S27S63S65S31D. SRAM-centric chips have single-digit balance points, which is their structural edge in small-batch, high-interactivity decode.

KV 缓存：推理里增长最快的那块内存The KV cache: the fastest-growing block of inference memory

Transformer 在生成每个新 token 时，要对之前所有 token 的 Key 与 Value 做注意力。为了不重复计算，这些 K/V 被缓存下来，这就是 KV 缓存。它的大小 = 2 × 层数 × KV 头数 × 头维度 × 每元素字节 × 上下文长度 × 并发数S38A。不同注意力结构差别巨大：When a Transformer generates each new token, it attends to the Keys and Values of all previous tokens. To avoid recomputing them, they are cached: the KV cache. Its size = 2 × layers × KV heads × head dim × bytes per element × context length × concurrencyS38A. Attention designs differ enormously:

每 token 的 KV 缓存（KiB，对数刻度）KV cache per token (KiB, log scale)
同为前沿模型，每 token KV 相差三个数量级以上；MLA 比同结构的多头注意力小约 71 倍Frontier models differ by over three orders of magnitude; MLA is ~71× smaller than the equivalent multi-head attention
数据表Data table
按模型配置计算（BF16，除注明外）：Llama 3.1 与 Qwen3 为 GQA，DeepSeek-V3 为 MLA（每层缓存 512 维潜向量 + 64 维 RoPE），GPT-OSS-120B 仅计全注意力层（另有每序列固定 4.5 MiB 滑窗缓存）S1S4S108D；V3 的 FP8 版按 FlashMLA 每层 656 字节S25A；V3.2 为 FP8 潜向量 + 索引器缓存S104C；V4-Flash 约 3,514 字节S103B；V4.1-Flash 为论文给出的全局 KV 890 字节S95A。Computed from model configs (BF16 unless noted): Llama 3.1 and Qwen3 use GQA; DeepSeek-V3 uses MLA (a 512-dim latent plus 64-dim RoPE per layer); GPT-OSS-120B counts full-attention layers only (plus a fixed 4.5 MiB sliding-window cache per sequence)S1S4S108D; V3 in FP8 uses FlashMLA's 656 bytes per layerS25A; V3.2 is an FP8 latent plus indexer cacheS104C; V4-Flash ~3,514 bytesS103B; V4.1-Flash is the paper's 890-byte global KVS95A.
算一笔账：为什么说 decode 是"读 KV"The arithmetic: why decode is mostly reading KV

B300 288 GB · 8 TB/s | Llama-70B FP8 weights ≈ 70 GB | 64 users × 8K ctx × 320 KiB = 171.8 GB KV | 241.8 GB per step → 33 steps/s → ~2,117 tok/s

在这个例子里，每一步要搬 241.8 GB，其中 71% 是 KV（若 KV 用 FP8 也还有 55%）；算力只用到约 0.34 PF，约为 FP8 峰值的 7%D。也就是说，这颗 GPU 大部分时间在等内存。上下文越长、并发越多，KV 的占比越高。In this example each step moves 241.8 GB, 71% of it KV (still 55% with FP8 KV); compute use is only ~0.34 PF, about 7% of FP8 peakD. The GPU spends most of its time waiting on memory. Longer contexts and more users raise KV's share further.

交互模型 M1Interactive model M1
KV 缓存与 decode 计算器：装得下多少用户？每秒能吐多少 token？KV cache and decode calculator: how many users fit, and how many tokens per second?
选择模型与硬件预设，或直接修改任一参数。decode 吞吐按"每步字节 ÷ 有效带宽"估算，忽略算力与通信限制，因此是带宽上限。Pick a model/hardware preset or edit any input. Decode throughput is estimated as effective bandwidth ÷ bytes per step, ignoring compute and communication limits, so it is a bandwidth ceiling.
预设Presets
GQA/MHA = 2 × KV 头 × 头维度（70B：2×8×128）；MLA = 576GQA/MHA = 2 × KV heads × head dim (70B: 2×8×128); MLA = 576
稠密 = 常驻；MoE 大 batch 时接近常驻，小 batch 时接近激活参数Dense = resident; MoE approaches resident at large batch and active params at small batch
实际常见 70–85%70–85% is typical in practice

KV/token = 层数 × 元素数 × 字节（或直接指定）。HBM 需求 = 常驻权重 + 全部 KV（另留 5% 余量）。每步字节 = 每步读取的权重 + 全部 KV。最大步频 = GPU 数 × 带宽 × 利用率 ÷ 每步字节；单用户 token/s ≈ 步频；总 token/s = 步频 × 用户数。超出 HBM 的 KV 需外溢到 CPU 内存或闪存，下方给出单个用户 KV 的回载时间。KV/token = layers × elements × bytes (or the override). HBM need = resident weights + all KV (plus 5% headroom). Bytes per step = weights read per step + all KV. Max step rate = GPUs × bandwidth × efficiency ÷ bytes per step; per-user tokens/s ≈ step rate; total tokens/s = step rate × users. KV beyond HBM spills to CPU memory or flash; reload times for one user's KV are shown.D

MoE：容量需求看总参数，带宽需求看激活参数MoE: capacity follows total parameters, bandwidth follows active ones
