S4S6S97S96S108S408S409AS4S6S97S96S108S408S409A

MoE 把"每 token 读多少权重"压到总参数的 1.4–10%，带宽压力下降，但所有专家都必须常驻在某块高速内存里：DeepSeek-V3 的 FP8 权重约 0.7 TB，Kimi K2 约 1 TBD。DeepSeek 的解法是把专家摊到大量 GPU 上：prefill 用 32 卡专家并行，decode 用 144 卡（每卡 2 个路由专家 + 1 个共享专家），理由是"每颗 GPU 只处理少数专家，降低内存访问需求"S23A。所以 MoE 不是减少 HBM 需求，而是把需求从"带宽"转向"容量 + 互联"。若 V4-Pro 的 1.6T 参数约 90% 以 FP4 存储，权重仍需约 0.9–1.0 TB，至少 4 颗 RubinS98D。MoE cuts the weights read per token to 1.4–10% of total parameters, easing bandwidth, but every expert must still sit in fast memory: DeepSeek-V3's FP8 weights are ~0.7 TB and Kimi K2's ~1 TBD. DeepSeek spreads experts over many GPUs: 32-way expert parallelism for prefill and 144-way for decode (two routed experts plus one shared per GPU), because "each GPU only needs to process a small subset of experts… reducing memory access demands"S23A. MoE does not cut HBM demand; it shifts it from bandwidth to capacity plus interconnect. If ~90% of V4-Pro's 1.6T parameters are stored in FP4, the weights still need ~0.9–1.0 TB, at least four RubinsS98D.

前缀缓存：KV 命中率成了推理的经济学Prefix caching: KV hit rate becomes the economics of inference

证据Evidence数据Data
llm-d 精确 KV 感知调度（16×H100）llm-d precise KV-aware scheduling (16×H100)P90 首 token 0.542 s vs 31.083 s（快 57 倍）；吞吐约翻倍；"缓存内 token 的处理成本低 10 倍（0.30 vs 3.00 美元/百万）"S77P90 TTFT 0.542 s vs 31.083 s (57× faster); ~2× throughput; "the cost for processing tokens already in cache is 10 times lower ($0.30 vs. $3.00 per million)"S77

DeepSeek V4.1-Flash 现价DeepSeek V4.1-Flash pricing缓存命中 0.006 美元/百万 vs 未命中 0.30 美元（50 倍）S99Cache hit $0.006/M vs miss $0.30/M (50×)S99
Anthropic APIAnthropic API缓存读取按基础输入价的 0.1 倍计费；写入 1.25 倍（5 分钟）或 2 倍（1 小时）S86Cache reads billed at 0.1× base input; writes 1.25× (5 min) or 2× (1 hour)S86

当缓存命中的 token 比未命中便宜 10–50 倍、首 token 延迟快几十倍时，"把 KV 留住"就成了推理服务商最重要的成本杠杆。代价是存储：每多保留一段上下文、多保留一分钟，就要多一份 KV 驻留在 HBM、CPU 内存或闪存里。这正是 NVIDIA 新增 G3.5 层、存储厂商推出"上下文内存"的原因。When cached tokens cost 10–50× less and first tokens arrive tens of times faster, keeping KV around becomes an inference provider's biggest cost lever. The price is storage: every extra stretch of context and every extra minute of retention means another copy of KV resident in HBM, CPU memory or flash. That is why NVIDIA added the G3.5 tier and storage vendors now sell "context memory".

重算还是回载？在 Llama-70B 上重新 prefill 一段 128K 提示约需 4.1×10¹⁶ 次运算（按 1 PF/s 有效算力约 41 秒），产生 42.9 GB 的 KV；把这些 KV 从主机内存回载（约 50 GB/s）只要约 0.9 秒——回载比重算快 10–48 倍D，与 vLLM、IBM、LMCache 的实测一致（见下图与下表）。Recompute or reload? Re-prefilling a 128K prompt on Llama-70B takes ~4.1×10¹⁶ operations (~41 s at 1 PF/s effective) and produces 42.9 GB of KV; reloading that KV from host memory (~50 GB/s) takes ~0.9 s, so reloading is 10–48× fasterD, consistent with vLLM, IBM and LMCache measurements (chart and table below).

分离式部署与 NVIDIA 的三块新拼图Disaggregated serving and NVIDIA's three new pieces

既然 prefill 与 decode 的瓶颈不同，就把它们拆到不同机器上（业内称为推理的“拆分”S204B；vLLM 已在大规模部署中采用宽专家并行与 PD 分离S75S76A）：DistServe 宣称可多服务 7.4 倍请求或把延迟目标收紧 12.6 倍S14A；Splitwise 在相同成本与功耗下吞吐 2.35 倍S121A；Kimi 的 Mooncake 以 KV 为中心，把集群闲置的 CPU、DRAM、SSD 组成 KV 池，模拟吞吐 +525%、生产中多处理 75% 请求S15A；NVIDIA Dynamo 在 GB200 NVL72 上让 DeepSeek-R1 的请求数最多提升 30 倍，其 KV 管理器按 GPU → CPU → 本地 SSD → 网络存储逐级外溢，"最多可达 PB 级 KV 数据"S39A。Since prefill and decode are bound differently, split them onto different machines (the industry calls it the unbundling of inferenceS204B; vLLM now runs wide expert parallelism and PD disaggregation at scaleS75S76A): DistServe claims 7.4× more requests or 12.6× tighter latency targetsS14A; Splitwise gets 2.35× throughput at the same cost and powerS121A; Kimi's KV-centric Mooncake pools idle CPU, DRAM and SSD for KV, reporting +525% throughput in simulation and 75% more requests in productionS15A; NVIDIA Dynamo lifts DeepSeek-R1 requests up to 30× on GB200 NVL72, with a KV manager spilling GPU → CPU → local SSD → networked storage, "up to petabytes of KV cache data"S39A.

CMX / STX

2026-01 CES 发布为 ICMS，3 月 GTC 以 CMX 之名作为 STX 参考架构的首个机架级实现；合作方包括 DDN、Dell、HPE、IBM、Nutanix、Pure、VAST、WEKA 等，2026 下半年可用S29S30S310A。它是 KV 需求进入 NAND 的"官方通道"。Announced at CES in Jan 2026 as ICMS and presented at GTC in March as CMX, the first rack-scale implementation of the STX reference architecture; partners include DDN, Dell, HPE, IBM, Nutanix, Pure, VAST and WEKA; available 2H26S29S30S310A. It is the official channel for KV demand into NAND.

Rubin CPX

2025-09 发布：128 GB GDDR7、30 PF NVFP4，专做长上下文 prefill，NVL144 CPX 机架有 100 TB 高速内存S33A。GTC 2026 路线图上消失S53B；郭明錤 9 月称或以 168 GB HBM4 版本回归、2027 年一季度量产（未证实）S54C。它说明"便宜内存做 prefill"的思路仍在摇摆。Announced in Sep 2025: 128 GB of GDDR7 and 30 PF NVFP4 for long-context prefill, with 100 TB of fast memory per NVL144 CPX rackS33A. It vanished from the GTC 2026 roadmapS53B; in September Ming-Chi Kuo reported a possible return with 168 GB of HBM4 and volume in 1Q27 (unverified)S54C. The cheap-memory-for-prefill idea is still in flux.

Groq 3 LPX

注意力（含 KV）留在 Rubin GPU，FFN/MoE 交给 LPU；每机架 128 GB SRAM + 12 TB DDR5S31S32S201A。NVIDIA 称支持 2T 参数模型与 40 万 token 的 KV 缓存S32A。它增加的是对高交互推理的投入，KV 容量需求仍落在 HBM。Attention (with KV) stays on Rubin GPUs while FFN/MoE goes to LPUs; each rack has 128 GB of SRAM plus 12 TB of DDR5S31S32S201A. NVIDIA cites support for 2T-parameter models with 400K KV-cached tokensS32A. It adds spending on interactive inference while KV capacity still lands on HBM.

AI 工作流 · AgentWorkloads · Agents

11

AI Agent：把瓶颈从"算力"推向"记忆"AI agents: pushing the bottleneck from compute to memory

Agent 与聊天的根本区别在于"循环"：它要规划、调用工具、读结果、再规划，几十上百轮地往同一个上下文里追加内容。每一轮都要把越来越长的上下文重新喂给模型（或命中缓存），工具执行时这些上下文又必须被"挂起"保存。NVIDIA 的说法是"上下文随每一轮 Agent 交互增长，注意力呈平方扩张"S58B。这让 Agent 的存储需求有三个特征：输入远多于输出、上下文持续增长、KV 必须跨分钟乃至小时保留。What separates agents from chat is the loop: plan, call a tool, read the result, plan again, appending to the same context for tens or hundreds of rounds. Each round re-feeds an ever-longer context to the model (or hits a cache), and while tools run that context must be parked. NVIDIA puts it as "context grows with every agent turn and attention expands quadratically"S58B. Agents' memory needs therefore have three traits: far more input than output, steadily growing context, and KV that must persist for minutes to hours.

Agent 相对聊天的 token 消耗Agent tokens vs chat4× / 15×单 Agent / 多智能体（Anthropic）S85Single agent / multi-agent (Anthropic)S85
输入:输出Input:output≈100:1Manus，约 50 次工具调用/任务S84Manus, ~50 tool calls per taskS84
缓存命中 vs 未命中Cached vs uncached便宜 10×10× cheaper0.30 vs 3 美元/百万 tokenS84S77$0.30 vs $3 per million tokensS84S77
谷歌 API token/分钟Google API tokens/min7B→22B3Q25 → 2Q26S80S793Q25 → 2Q26S80S79

Agent 工作负载有何不同How agent workloads differ

  • 更多 token：Anthropic 的多智能体研究系统中，token 用量解释了 80% 的性能差异；Agent 会把计划写进记忆，因为上下文"超过 20 万 token"S85A。More tokens: in Anthropic's multi-agent research system, token usage explained 80% of performance variance, and agents saved their plans to memory because context "exceeds 200,000 tokens"S85A.
  • KV 命中率是第一指标：Manus 称"KV 缓存命中率是生产级 Agent 最重要的单一指标"，并把文件系统当作"无限的上下文"S84A。KV hit rate is the first metric: Manus calls KV-cache hit rate "the single most important metric for a production-stage AI agent" and uses the file system as unlimited contextS84A.
  • 更长的任务：Kimi K2 Thinking 提供 256K 上下文、可连续 200–300 次工具调用S410B；推理模型每题输出 token 自 2H24 起每年增长 5 倍以上，现约 3–4 万S88B。Longer tasks: Kimi K2 Thinking offers 256K context and 200–300 sequential tool callsS410B; reasoning output per question has grown more than 5× a year since 2H24 and is now ~30–40K tokensS88B.
  • 更长的提示：OpenRouter 平均提示从约 1.5K 增至 6K 以上，编程占 token 从 11% 升至 50% 以上，推理模型承载超过一半 tokenS82B；2026 年 8 月 Agent 约占其 token 的 71%，每日 Agent token 从 0.51 万亿增至 7.3 万亿（2–8 月）S83C。Longer prompts: OpenRouter's average prompt grew from ~1.5K to 6K+ tokens, programming rose from 11% to over 50% of tokens, and reasoning models carry over halfS82B; by Aug 2026 agents were ~71% of its tokens, with daily agent tokens rising from 0.51T to 7.3T between February and AugustS83C.
  • 更多 CPU：工具执行与编排让 CPU:GPU 比例走向 1:4 乃至 1:2S87B，微软称其平台上已有"数千万个 Agent"、token 处理量"环比加速 30%"S81A。More CPU: tool execution and orchestration push CPU:GPU ratios towards 1:4 or 1:2S87B; Microsoft reports "tens of millions of agents" and token processing "accelerating 30% quarter over quarter"S81A.

Agent 的"记忆分层"An agent's memory hierarchy

Agent KV 落层计算器：一个 NVL72 机架需要多少 HBM、内存和闪存？Agent KV placement calculator: how much HBM, memory and flash does one NVL72 rack need?
同时选"负载"和"模型 KV 结构"。它回答一个争议问题：花旗的"每 GPU 16 TB 闪存"在什么假设下成立？DeepSeek V4.1 式压缩会把它压到多少？Choose a workload and a model KV structure. It answers a live question: under what assumptions does Citi's "16 TB of flash per GPU" hold, and how far would V4.1-style compression cut it?

口径：活跃 KV = 活跃会话 × 上下文 × 每 token 字节，先放 HBM；挂起 KV = 活跃会话 × 挂起倍数 × 上下文 × 每 token 字节，先放 CPU 内存，HBM 溢出部分也进入 CPU 内存；共享前缀与 CPU 内存溢出部分进入闪存（CMX/NVMe）。每 GPU 闪存 = 机架闪存 ÷ 72，对照花旗每 GPU 16 TBS50S51。负载参数为示意，不代表任何公司的实际流量。Basis: active KV = active sessions × context × bytes per token, placed in HBM first; paused KV = active × paused multiple × context × bytes, placed in CPU memory, which also takes HBM overflow; shared prefixes and CPU-memory overflow go to flash (CMX/NVMe). Flash per GPU = rack flash ÷ 72, compared with Citi's 16 TB per GPUS50S51. Workload parameters are illustrative, not any company's real traffic.D

Agent 最压哪几层（排序）Which tiers agents stress most (ranked)

  1. HBM 容量与带宽。HBM capacity and bandwidth. 长且不断增长的上下文 × 并发决定容量；decode 时 KV 读取主导流量（§10）。谷歌 TPU 8i 把 384 MB SRAM"按推理模型的 KV 占用来设计"，说明 KV 已成为芯片设计的第一约束S66S67AB。Long, growing contexts × concurrency set capacity, and KV reads dominate decode traffic (§10). Google sized TPU 8i's 384 MB SRAM "for the KV cache footprint of reasoning models", showing KV has become a first-order chip design constraintS66S67AB.
  2. CPU 侧 DRAM（LPDDR5X / DDR5）。CPU-side DRAM (LPDDR5X / DDR5). 挂起会话的 KV、工具执行与编排都在这里；NVIDIA 只拿到约 60% 想要的 LPDDR，Vera Rubin 的 SOCAMM 因此减半S214B。Parked-session KV, tool execution and orchestration all live here; NVIDIA is getting only ~60% of the LPDDR it wants, so Vera Rubin's SOCAMM was halvedS214B.
  3. 闪存（G3 本地、G3.5 CMX）。Flash (G3 local, G3.5 CMX). 跨分钟到小时保留并在副本间共享 KV，经济动因是命中 token 便宜约 10 倍。NVIDIA："这是几乎所有大量生成 token 的人的痛点"S413B。Keeps and shares KV across minutes to hours, driven by cached tokens being ~10× cheaper. NVIDIA: "this is a pain point for just about everybody who does a lot of token generation today"S413B.
  4. HDD 与对象存储。HDD and object storage. 保存 Agent 每一步产生的数据与轨迹，供审计、评测和下一轮训练使用。Store the data and trajectories every agent step creates, for audit, evaluation and the next round of training.
  5. 片上 SRAM。On-chip SRAM. 交互式 Agent 需要每用户高 token/s，SRAM 派推理（LPX、TPU 8i、Maia 200）在这一点上有优势S31S66S69A。Interactive agents need high tokens/s per user, where SRAM-centric inference (LPX, TPU 8i, Maia 200) has an edgeS31S66S69A.

核心问题 Q1c 的完整回答The full answer to Q1c

12

瓶颈总矩阵：每个阶段卡在哪、最缺什么、往哪走、怎么绕The bottleneck matrix: where each stage is bound, what is scarcest, where it goes, how to route around

下表把训练、推理与 Agent 的 21 个阶段放在一起。"压力"为 2026 年的综合判断；"对存储需求的净效应"指绕行路径普及后，对该层存储总需求的方向（↑ 增加、↓ 减少、⇄ 层间转移、= 基本不变）。The table puts all 21 stages of training, inference and agents together. "Pressure" is our 2026 assessment; "net effect on memory demand" is the direction of total demand for that tier once workarounds spread (↑ more, ↓ less, ⇄ shifted between tiers, = little change).D

从矩阵读出的四个规律Four patterns in the matrix
  1. HBM 在 6 个阶段是"关键"（T4、T7、I5、I6、A1、A6），且全部与 decode 或容量有关——这是 HBM 需求的底盘。HBM is critical in six stages (T4, T7, I5, I6, A1, A6), all tied to decode or capacity: the base of HBM demand.
  2. CPU 侧 DRAM 在 8 个阶段承压，多数是 Agent 与容错带来的"新需求"，而非传统服务器。CPU-side DRAM is pressured in eight stages, mostly new demand from agents and fault tolerance rather than traditional servers.
  3. NAND 的高压力集中在"保留"：checkpoint、多轮 KV、RAG——它对 KV 结构最敏感。NAND's high pressure concentrates in retention: checkpoints, multi-turn KV and RAG; it is the most sensitive to KV structure.
  4. HDD 的压力来自数据的产生与留存，与算法效率几乎无关。HDD pressure comes from data creation and retention and is nearly independent of algorithmic efficiency.
不要误读Do not misread

"关键"不等于"越多越好"：decode 的瓶颈是带宽，HBM 容量翻倍不会让单用户速度翻倍；KV 外溢层的瓶颈是"每 GB·小时的成本"，所以 QLC 胜过 TLC。不同阶段对同一层存储要的指标不同——容量、带宽、延迟、每 bit 成本、寿命，这决定了每一层未来往哪个方向优化。"Critical" does not mean "more is always better". Decode is bound by bandwidth, so doubling HBM capacity does not double per-user speed; KV spill tiers are bound by cost per GB-hour, so QLC beats TLC. Different stages want different things from the same tier (capacity, bandwidth, latency, cost per bit, endurance), and that sets the direction in which each tier will be optimized.

绕行与演进Workarounds

13

能否绕开存储墙？三类路径、一个案例、一场拔河Can we route around the memory wall? Three families, one case study, one tug-of-war

绕开存储瓶颈的办法可以分三类：算法（让每个 token 需要的存储更少）、架构（换一种存储或换一种组织方式）、系统（让同一份存储被更多请求复用）。评价每一种办法要问同一个问题：它是"消灭"了需求，还是把需求"搬"到了另一层？下面逐类给出证据，然后用 DeepSeek V4.1-Flash 做案例，最后用一个交互模型看压缩与需求增长谁跑得快。Workarounds fall into three families: algorithmic (less memory per token), architectural (a different memory or a different arrangement) and systems (the same memory reused by more requests). For each, ask one question: does it remove demand, or move it to another tier? Evidence by family follows, then DeepSeek V4.1-Flash as a case study, then an interactive model of whether compression or demand growth runs faster.

一、算法路径：每个 token 少用一点1. Algorithmic paths: less memory per token

二、架构与系统路径：换一种存储，或换一种组织2. Architectural and systems paths: a different memory, or a different arrangement

SRAMSRAM 派推理SRAM-centric inference

LPX 用 128 GB SRAM 承接 FFN/MoE 权重流，宣称每兆瓦吞吐 35 倍；KV 仍在 HBMS31A。效果：HBM 的权重读取部分转移，KV 不动；LP40 起加入堆叠 DRAMS57B。LPX streams FFN/MoE weights from 128 GB of SRAM, claiming 35× throughput per megawatt; KV stays in HBMS31A. Effect: some HBM weight reads move, KV does not; stacked DRAM arrives with LP40S57B.

P/Dprefill/decode 分离Prefill/decode split

DistServe 7.4 倍、Dynamo 最高 30 倍S14S39A。效果：提高 HBM 利用率，同时新增 KV 传输与外溢层的需求；GDDR7 版 CPX 曾试图用便宜内存做 prefillS33A。DistServe 7.4×, Dynamo up to 30×S14S39A. Effect: better HBM utilization plus new demand for KV transfer and spill tiers; the GDDR7 CPX tried cheaper memory for prefillS33A.

CXL池化与光互连Pooling and optics

Pond 降低 DRAM 成本 7%S122A；Marvell 48 TB 池、Celestial 32 TB 光互连池S123S126B。效果：bit 仍在 DRAM，利用率提高、更多内存挂到推理系统。Pond cut DRAM cost 7%S122A; Marvell's 48 TB pools and Celestial's 32 TB optical poolsS123S126B. Effect: bits stay in DRAM, utilization rises, more memory gets attached to inference.

HBF高带宽闪存High Bandwidth Flash

8–16 倍 HBM 容量、相近成本，闪迪称 4 颗配 HBF 的 GPU 抵 8 颗纯 HBM GPUS133S134AB。效果：唯一可能把部分 HBM 容量需求搬到 NAND 的路线，2028 年量产。8–16× HBM capacity at a similar cost; SanDisk says 4 GPUs with HBF match 8 HBM-only GPUsS133S134AB. Effect: the only route that could move some HBM capacity demand to NAND; volume in 2028.

3DDRAM 叠在逻辑上DRAM stacked on logic

d-Matrix Raptor 每卡 100 TB/s、0.37 pJ/bitS131B；三星规划 HBM 直接叠在计算裸片上S132B。效果：从标准 HBM 转向定制堆叠 DRAM，仍是 DRAM。d-Matrix Raptor: 100 TB/s per card at 0.37 pJ/bitS131B; Samsung plans HBM stacked directly on computeS132B. Effect: from standard HBM to custom stacked DRAM, still DRAM.

KV前缀缓存与分层Prefix caching and tiering

SGLang RadixAttention 吞吐最高 6.4 倍；LMCache 最高 15 倍；DeepSeek 56.3% 输入命中磁盘缓存S17S16S23A。效果：省下重复的 prefill 算力，把需求从 HBM 搬到 DRAM 与闪存。SGLang RadixAttention up to 6.4× throughput; LMCache up to 15×; DeepSeek served 56.3% of input from disk cacheS17S16S23A. Effect: saves repeated prefill compute and moves demand from HBM into DRAM and flash.

需求影响矩阵：每条路径对各层存储的方向Demand-impact matrix: each path's direction for each tier

代码：R 降低单位需求；R* 降低每参数需求，但历史上被再投入到更大的模型；S− 需求移出该层；S+ 需求移入该层；I 增加需求；N 基本无影响。Codes: R cuts unit demand; R* cuts demand per parameter, historically reinvested in bigger models; S− demand moves out of the tier; S+ demand moves in; I raises demand; N little effect.D

案例：DeepSeek V4.1-Flash 到底说了什么Case study: what DeepSeek V4.1-Flash actually claimed

2026-09-10，DeepSeek 发布 V4.1-Flash，称相对上一代"HBM 用量 1/4、SSD 存储 1/8"S94A。9-17 的论文给出精确口径：全局 KV 缓存（"始终在 HBM 中"）降到每 token 890 字节，约为 V4-Flash 的 1/4；持久化 KV 缓存（"始终在 SSD 或主机内存中"）约为 V4-Flash 的 1/8S95A。手段包括带跨层 KV 复用的 CSA2、FP4（E2M1）存储主 KV，以及对滑窗层只重放最后一个窗口S95S96A。你在 9-11 的专题研报里已讨论过"HBM 用量会不会减少"，这里补充截至今天的证据与边界。On Sep 10, 2026, DeepSeek released V4.1-Flash, claiming "1/4 the HBM" and "1/8 the SSD storage" of the previous generationS94A. The Sep 17 paper is precise: the global KV cache ("always in HBM") falls to 890 bytes per token, about 1/4 of V4-Flash; the persistent KV cache ("always on SSD or in host memory") to about 1/8 of V4-FlashS95A. The methods include CSA2 with cross-layer KV reuse, FP4 (E2M1) storage of the main KV, and replaying only the last window for sliding-window layersS95S96A. Your Sep 11 report already discussed whether HBM usage will fall; here is the evidence and its limits as of today.

DeepSeek 历代每 token KV（字节，对数刻度）DeepSeek KV per token by generation (bytes, log scale)

它没有说什么（边界）What it did not claim (limits)
  1. 基准是 DeepSeek 自己 4 月发布的 V4-Flash，不是 V4-Pro，也不是其他厂商的模型。The baseline is DeepSeek's own April V4-Flash, not V4-Pro and not other vendors' models.
  2. 只涉及 KV 缓存，不涉及权重（"模型权重仍需要存储与访问"）S103B。Only the KV cache is affected, not the weights ("model weights still need storage and access")S103B.
  3. 890 字节是"全局"缓存；滑窗层的局部 KV 另按窗口大小计D。890 bytes is the "global" cache; sliding-window layers' local KV is bounded separately by the windowD.
  4. 发布稿里的"SSD"在论文中是"SSD 或主机内存"，持久层包括 CPU 内存。"SSD" in the release is "SSD or host memory" in the paper, so the persistent tier includes CPU memory.
  5. 所有基准为自报：Terminal-Bench 2.1 90.6 vs 82.7，DeepSWE 74.2 vs 54.4S96A。All benchmarks are self-reported: Terminal-Bench 2.1 90.6 vs 82.7, DeepSWE 74.2 vs 54.4S96A.
市场反应Market reaction

9-11 首尔收盘：三星 −3.53%、SK 海力士 −2.21%、KOSPI −1.76%；东京：铠侠 −6.99%S102B。美股美光、闪迪盘中约 −4%，但收盘口径各源不一S102S401C。三星证券称这"更像是对情绪的压制，而不是对 HBM 需求的根本重估"S102B。Sep 11 closes in Seoul: Samsung −3.53%, SK hynix −2.21%, KOSPI −1.76%; in Tokyo Kioxia −6.99%S102B. In the US, Micron and SanDisk fell ~4% intraday, though sources disagree on the closeS102S401C. Samsung Securities called it "more like a dampener on market sentiment rather than a fundamental repricing of HBM demand"S102B.

拔河：压缩速度 vs 需求增速The tug-of-war: compression versus demand growth

历史数据：DeepSeek 每 token KV 从 V3（BF16）到 V4.1 约 21 个月缩小 79 倍，约 12 倍/年（对比 FP8 版 V3 约 8 倍/年）S95D；同期每单位能力的 token 价格每年下降约 10 倍（a16z）或 9–900 倍（Epoch，视基准而定）S93S92B。需求侧：谷歌月度 token 约 16 倍/年S90D，默认上下文从 128K 到 1MS97A。这与 Jevons 悖论一致——效率提升带来更多使用——但不能证明它，因为 token 序列混合了不同产品，KV 序列只来自一家实验室。下面的模型把这场拔河简化为三个变量。History: DeepSeek's KV per token fell 79× from V3 (BF16) to V4.1 in ~21 months, ~12× a year (~8× a year against an FP8 V3)S95D; over the same period token prices at constant capability fell ~10× a year (a16z) or 9–900× (Epoch, by benchmark)S93S92B. Demand: Google's monthly tokens grew ~16× a yearS90D and default contexts went from 128K to 1MS97A. This is consistent with the Jevons paradox (efficiency brings more use) but does not prove it: the token series mixes products and the KV series comes from one lab. The model below reduces the tug-of-war to three variables.

交互模型 M4Interactive model M4
Jevons 拔河：KV 容量需求会涨还是会跌？Jevons tug-of-war: will KV capacity demand rise or fall?
KV 容量需求 ∝ 并发会话 × 保留上下文 × 每 token 字节。假设每会话的生成速度不变，并发随 token 吞吐同比增长。KV capacity demand ∝ concurrent sessions × retained context × bytes per token. Assumes per-session speed is constant, so concurrency grows with token throughput.
预设Presets
1 = 不缩小；DeepSeek 历史约 8–121 = no reduction; DeepSeek's history ~8–12
只有部分模型采用时，有效缩小倍数会打折If only some models adopt it, the effective reduction is diluted

年变化 =（1+token 增速）×（1+上下文增速）÷ 有效缩小倍数 − 1；有效缩小倍数 = 1 ÷ [普及率 ÷ r + (1 − 普及率)]。盈亏平衡缩小倍数 =（1+token 增速）×（1+上下文增速）。"2024–26 风格化"按谷歌 token 约 16 倍/年、默认上下文约 2.8 倍/年、KV 每年缩小约 10 倍设定，用于说明过去两年需求跑赢了压缩，并非预测。Annual change = (1 + token growth) × (1 + context growth) ÷ effective reduction − 1; effective reduction = 1 ÷ [adoption ÷ r + (1 − adoption)]. Break-even reduction = (1 + token growth) × (1 + context growth). The "2024–26 stylized" preset uses Google's ~16× tokens a year, ~2.8× default context a year and ~10× KV reduction a year to show demand outran compression over the past two years; it is not a forecast.D

判断Judgment

绕行路径会持续削减"每 token、每参数"的存储，但到目前为止，被省下的存储基本都被更长的上下文、更多的并发会话和更大的模型重新吃掉。三层的风险不同：HBM——带宽需求跟 token 量走，容量需求被 KV 压缩部分抵消，总体仍上升；CPU 侧 DRAM——受益于 Agent 与外溢分层，压缩影响有限；NAND 的 KV 部分——对模型结构最敏感，是最可能被"算法一夜改写"的一块，也是 2026–27 年预测分歧最大的一块。Workarounds will keep cutting memory per token and per parameter, but so far the savings have been eaten by longer contexts, more concurrent sessions and bigger models. The risk differs by tier. HBM: bandwidth demand follows token volume while KV compression partly offsets capacity, so the total still rises. CPU-side DRAM: benefits from agents and spill tiers, with limited exposure to compression. NAND for KV: the most sensitive to model architecture, the piece most likely to be rewritten overnight by an algorithm, and the piece with the widest forecast spread for 2026–27.

供需、价格与产能：2026 涨得最猛，2027 最紧，2028 起放量Supply, demand, prices and capacity: 2026 rose fastest, 2027 is tightest, volume arrives from 2028

市场规模：预测一路追着价格上调Market size: forecasts keep chasing prices up

全球存储营收预测（十亿美元）与占半导体比例Global memory revenue forecasts (US$bn) and share of semiconductors
柱为各机构预测；折线为 WSTS 口径的存储占半导体营收比例（右轴）Bars are forecasts by source; the line is WSTS's memory share of chip revenue (right axis)
数据表Data table
TrendForce（5 月）S87、Gartner（8 月）S327、WSTS（6 月）S325。WSTS 8 月更新已把 2026 年存储增速上调至 +302%（约 9,250 亿美元）S326D。交叉验证：2Q26 DRAM 营收 1,547 亿美元 × 4 ≈ 6,190 亿美元，几乎等于 TrendForce 全年 DRAM 预测（6,187 亿美元），而 3、4 季度价格仍在上涨，说明 Gartner 与 WSTS 的 2026 年数字偏低S207D。TrendForce (May)S87, Gartner (Aug)S327, WSTS (Jun)S325. WSTS's August update raised 2026 memory growth to +302% (~$925bn)S326D. Cross-check: 2Q26 DRAM revenue of $154.7bn × 4 ≈ $619bn, almost exactly TrendForce's full-year DRAM forecast ($618.7bn), while 3Q and 4Q prices are still rising, so Gartner's and WSTS's 2026 figures look lowS207D.

需要警惕的是方向：几乎所有机构都在"追着价格"上调预测，一旦价格回落，下调也会同样剧烈。以 TrendForce 为例：1 月预测 2026 年存储营收 5,516 亿美元，5 月上调到 8,893 亿美元S217S87B；南亚 7 月给出的口径约 8,000 亿美元S343B。WSTS 口径下 2026 年存储（8,039 亿美元）约为逻辑芯片（4,114 亿美元）的 2 倍S325A；Gartner 称 2026 年 AI 数据中心芯片约占半导体营收 36.5%，2030 年约 53%S327B。Watch the direction: nearly every forecaster is chasing prices upward, and cuts will be just as sharp when prices roll over. TrendForce is an example: in January it forecast 2026 memory revenue at $551.6bn and in May raised it to $889.3bnS217S87B; Nanya's July figure was about $800bnS343B. On WSTS's basis, 2026 memory ($803.9bn) is about twice logic ($411.4bn)S325A; Gartner puts AI data-center chips at ~36.5% of 2026 chip revenue, heading to ~53% by 2030S327B.

现货与合约Spot versus contract

10 月 6 日 DRAMeXchange 现货：DDR4 8Gb 46.32 美元，约为 9 月合约价（26 美元，2016 年以来最高）的 1.8 倍；DDR5 16Gb 58.17 美元；DDR5 RDIMM 32GB 模组现货 2,163.5 美元（9 月 28 日）S254S253B。但 9 月中旬 DDR5 现货出现"早期松动迹象"，TrendForce 认为据此判断合约价下跌"为时过早"S414C。DRAMeXchange spot on Oct 6: DDR4 8Gb at $46.32, ~1.8× September's contract price ($26, the highest since 2016); DDR5 16Gb at $58.17; DDR5 RDIMM 32GB module spot at $2,163.5 (Sep 28)S254S253B. But DDR5 spot showed "early signs of softening" in mid-September, and TrendForce says it is "premature" to read that as contract declinesS414C.

斜率比水平更重要The slope matters more than the level

美光的季度营收环比增速从 +74% 到 +31% 再到指引的 +13%；三星三季度一致预期自 8 月底下调 7.7%，原因之一是长约价格上限S161S332AC。价格仍在涨，但利润增速可能在 2027 年上半年先见顶——这是存储股在基本面强劲时仍大幅波动的主要原因（见 §18）。Micron's sequential revenue growth went from +74% to +31% to a guided +13%; Samsung's Q3 consensus has been cut 7.7% since late August, partly on LTA price capsS161S332AC. Prices are still rising, but profit growth may peak in 1H27, the main reason memory stocks swing hard even with strong fundamentals (see §18).

一个吉瓦的 AI 工厂要多少存储How much memory one gigawatt of AI factory needs

NVIDIA 称 100 MW 可容纳 4 万颗 Rubin GPU；Hot Chips 2026 上给出的口径是每 100 MW 约 11 PB HBM4、800 PB/s 带宽S27S58AB。据此每吉瓦约 40 万颗 GPU、约 5,556 个 NVL72 机架、110–115 PB HBM4D。下面的模型把 HBM、CPU 内存、上下文闪存与本地 NVMe 一起算进来，并换算成金额与全球供给占比。NVIDIA says 100 MW holds 40,000 Rubin GPUs; at Hot Chips 2026 it cited ~11 PB of HBM4 and 800 PB/s per 100 MWS27S58AB. So a gigawatt holds ~400,000 GPUs, ~5,556 NVL72 racks and 110–115 PB of HBM4D. The model below adds CPU memory, context flash and local NVMe, and converts them to dollars and shares of global supply.

交互模型 M6Interactive model M6
每吉瓦 Vera Rubin 的存储账单The memory bill for one gigawatt of Vera Rubin
默认：2027 年 HBM4 约 36 美元/GB、SOCAMM 按减配后的每机架 28 TB、花旗的每 GPU 16 TB 上下文闪存。Defaults: 2027 HBM4 at ~$36/GB, SOCAMM at the reduced 28 TB per rack, Citi's 16 TB of context flash per GPU.
预设Presets

机架数 = GPU ÷ 72。供给占比的分母：2026 年 HBM 约 3.75 EBS169、DRAM 约 40 EBS368、NAND 约 1.24 ZB（花旗隐含）S297。LPDDR5X 每 GB 价格按 TrendForce"每机架 120 万美元（54 TB）/58.6 万美元（28 TB）"折算约 21–22 美元S344D。Racks = GPUs ÷ 72. Supply-share denominators: 2026 HBM ~3.75 EBS169, DRAM ~40 EBS368, NAND ~1.24 ZB (Citi-implied)S297. LPDDR5X at ~$21–22/GB from TrendForce's "$1.2M per rack (54 TB) / $586K (28 TB)"S344D.D

判断Judgment

每吉瓦的存储账单在百亿美元量级，其中 HBM 与上下文闪存可能各占三分之一左右，但闪存的那三分之一几乎完全取决于"每 GPU 多少 TB"这个假设——它在 M3 里会随模型 KV 结构变化 1–2 个数量级。如果全球每年新增 20 吉瓦 Rubin 级算力，仅 HBM 就需要约 2.3 EB，约为 2026 年 HBM 总产出的 60%D——这就是 HBM 需要连续数年 +50% 以上 bit 增长的原因。The memory bill per gigawatt is on the order of $10bn, with HBM and context flash perhaps a third each, but the flash third depends almost entirely on the TB-per-GPU assumption, which M3 shows can swing by one to two orders of magnitude with the model's KV structure. If the world adds 20 GW of Rubin-class compute a year, HBM alone needs ~2.3 EB, about 60% of 2026 HBM outputD, which is why HBM needs 50%+ bit growth for several years running.

各层的价值归谁、护城河在哪Who captures each tier's value, and where the moats are

设备商：存储是 2026–27 年晶圆设备增长最快的部分Equipment: memory is the fastest-growing part of wafer equipment in 2026–27

  • 泛林 6 月季度系统营收中存储占 46%（NAND 23%、DRAM 23%），此前为 39%；预计 DRAM 是 2027 年增长最快的设备细分S359B。Lam: memory was 46% of June-quarter systems revenue (NAND 23%, DRAM 23%), up from 39%; it expects DRAM to be the fastest-growing equipment segment in 2027S359B.
  • 应用材料 FQ3 FY26：芯片系统收入中 DRAM 占 26%、闪存 7%S360A。ASML：2026 年存储收入 +75%，先进逻辑 +25%；2027 年 EUV 产能 +30%S361A。Applied Materials FQ3 FY26: DRAM 26% and flash 7% of chip-systems revenueS360A. ASML: memory revenue +75% in 2026 versus +25% for advanced logic; EUV capacity +30% in 2027S361A.
  • HBM 后道：韩美 2Q26 营收 2,511 亿韩元、营业利润率 51.9%，TC 键合机份额 71.2%S367S422B；BESI 2Q26 订单 2.929 亿欧元（同比 +128.8%）S193A；Yole 预计 TC 键合机市场 2025–2030 年从 5.42 亿增至 9.36 亿美元、混合键合 1.52 亿增至 3.97 亿美元S195B。HBM back end: Hanmi's 2Q26 revenue was ₩251.1bn at a 51.9% operating margin with a 71.2% share of TC bondersS367S422B; BESI's 2Q26 orders were €292.9M (+128.8% YoY)S193A; Yole sees TC bonders growing from $542M to $936M and hybrid bonding from $152M to $397M over 2025–2030S195B.

你可能忽略的点（Q2）What you may have missed (Q2)

16

被忽略的关键问题：十四个容易看漏的变量What is easy to miss: fourteen overlooked variables

前面各节回答了"哪一层缺、为什么缺"。这一节补上决定周期长度与利润分配、但常被技术讨论忽略的变量。每一条都给出数据、它为什么重要、以及该看什么。Earlier sections answered which tier is short and why. This one adds the variables that decide the cycle's length and how profits are split, which technical discussions often skip. Each item gives the data, why it matters and what to watch.

  1. 看斜率，不只看水平。Watch the slope, not just the level. DRAM 合约价环比从 1Q26 的 +93–98% 降到 4Q26 的 +10–15%S206S209B；美光营收环比 +74% → +31% → 指引 +13%S161AD；三星三季度一致预期自 8 月底下调 7.7%，高盛从 112 万亿下调到 106 万亿韩元S332S333C。价格仍在涨，利润增速可能先在 1H27 见顶。看：每季度 TrendForce 合约价预测、三星 10-08 初步业绩。DRAM contract price growth fell from +93–98% QoQ in 1Q26 to +10–15% in 4Q26S206S209B; Micron's sequential revenue growth went +74% → +31% → a guided +13%S161AD; Samsung's Q3 consensus has been cut 7.7% since late August, with Goldman moving from ₩112tn to ₩106tnS332S333C. Prices still rise, but profit growth may peak first, in 1H27. Watch: quarterly TrendForce forecasts; Samsung's Oct 8 prelim.
  2. 长约是双刃剑。LTAs cut both ways. 预付款从不到 5% 升至合同额的 10–30%S257B；美光 FQ4 收到 123 亿美元预付款，26 份协议中约 75% 有价格区间（下限与上限）S258S161BA；新合同把短缺量从预付款中扣除，而 2019 年的合同可以单方面取消S423C。但 TrendForce 指出长约"限制了供应商涨价"，三星三季度预期下调部分归因于此S213S332BC。长约降低了下行的深度，也削平了上行的尖峰；它在下行周期中的执行力尚未经过检验。Prepayments rose from under 5% to 10–30% of contract valueS257B; Micron received $12.3bn of advance payments in FQ4, and ~75% of its 26 agreements carry price bands with floors and ceilingsS258S161BA; new contracts deduct shortfalls from prepayments, whereas 2019-era contracts could be cancelled unilaterallyS423C. But TrendForce says LTAs "restrict suppliers from raising prices", and Samsung's Q3 cut was partly blamed on themS213S332BC. LTAs make downturns shallower and upturns flatter; their enforcement in a downturn is untested.
  3. 买家正在"设计掉"存储。Buyers are designing memory out. VR200 系统中存储约占 BOM 29%，NVIDIA 希望不超过约 20%S344B；Vera Rubin 的 SOCAMM 容量减半，每机架 LPDDR5X 从约 120 万美元降到约 58.6 万美元S214S344B；Rubin Ultra 可能从 1 TB 降到 384 GB 并考虑 8 层堆栈S377S166CB；NVIDIA 同时把服务器涨价 15% 以上S182B；CSP 把 RDIMM 从 96/128GB 换成 32/64GB，PC 品牌削减 SSD 容量S213S209B。需求对价格是有弹性的。Memory is ~29% of a VR200 system's BOM and NVIDIA wants it at ~20% or lessS344B; Vera Rubin's SOCAMM was halved, cutting LPDDR5X per rack from ~$1.2M to ~$586KS214S344B; Rubin Ultra may drop from 1 TB to 384 GB and weigh 8-high stacksS377S166CB; NVIDIA is also raising server prices more than 15%S182B; CSPs swap 96/128GB RDIMMs for 32/64GB and PC brands cut SSD capacityS213S209B. Demand is price-elastic.
  4. 消费电子的需求正在被摧毁。Consumer demand is being destroyed. 见下图。iPhone 18 Pro 的存储成本占 BOM 约 34%，1H27 将超过 40%S220B；笔电的 CPU+DRAM+SSD 占 BOM 从 45% 升到 68%，要维持 1Q25 的毛利需涨价约 80%S218B；PS5 涨 100 美元、Switch 2 涨 50 美元S263B；三星手机部门 2Q26 亏损 0.7 万亿韩元，闪迪消费业务环比 −32%S336S292A。手机与笔电仍占 NAND bit 需求近 40%S269B，而提前备货会把一部分下滑推迟到 2027 年。See the chart. Memory is ~34% of the iPhone 18 Pro's BOM and will pass 40% in 1H27S220B; notebooks' CPU+DRAM+SSD share of BOM rose from 45% to 68%, so holding 1Q25 margins would need ~80% higher pricesS218B; PS5 rose $100 and Switch 2 $50S263B; Samsung's phone division lost ₩0.7tn in 2Q26 and SanDisk's consumer revenue fell 32% QoQS336S292A. Phones and notebooks are still ~40% of NAND bit demandS269B, and pull-forward buying pushes part of the decline into 2027.
  5. 库存与重复下单。Inventory and double-ordering. KB 证券估三星与 SK 海力士库存低于 10 天，这是券商估计，公司并不披露S252C；美光库存 129 天，含停产节点的提前备货S161A。当客户只能拿到 50–66% 的需求时，就有动机重复下单或囤货；TrendForce 也提到 2Q26 因 CPU 短缺，美国 CSP 的 DRAM 库存逐步累积S213B。重复订单在供给缓解时会迅速撤回。需求端的“意向”也要打折：2025 年 10 月 OpenAI 称三星与 SK 海力士将为 Stargate 供应最多每月 90 万片 DRAM 晶圆投片S89A，按 Citrini 的 2026 年产能估算约相当于全行业的 38%S250D——这是意向书，不是订单。KB Securities estimates Samsung and SK hynix inventories below 10 days, a broker estimate the companies do not discloseS252C; Micron holds 129 days, including end-of-life build-aheadS161A. When customers receive only 50–66% of what they want, they have reason to double-order or stockpile; TrendForce also noted US CSP DRAM inventory building in 2Q26 because of CPU shortagesS213B. Double orders vanish quickly once supply eases. Demand-side intentions deserve a haircut too: in Oct 2025 OpenAI said Samsung and SK hynix would supply up to 900,000 DRAM wafer starts a month for StargateS89A, roughly 38% of industry capacity on Citrini's 2026 estimatesS250D; that is a letter of intent, not an order.
  6. HBM 的护城河在变薄。HBM's moat is thinning. 1Q26 每片晶圆做 DDR5 RDIMM 的收入超过 HBMS167B；三星 HBM 份额从 21% 升到 33%，三家都已通过 NVIDIA 认证S188S179B。2027 年的 +121% 重定价与长约锁定掩盖了这一点，但 2028 年后随着良率成熟与产能释放，HBM 可能从"卖方市场"转向价格竞争。In 1Q26 a wafer of DDR5 RDIMM earned more than a wafer of HBMS167B; Samsung's HBM share rose from 21% to 33% and all three makers have passed NVIDIA qualificationS188S179B. The 2027 +121% repricing and LTAs mask this, but after 2028, as yields mature and capacity lands, HBM may turn from a seller's market to price competition.
  7. 价值在流向台积电。Value is flowing to TSMC. HBM4 起基底裸片转向晶圆代工逻辑，定制 HBM4E 把内存控制器搬进基底；台积电基底据报为 SK 海力士核心裸片成本的 3–4 倍，SK 海力士据报评估英特尔作为第二来源（公司称部分报道不实）S172S173S183S424B。存储厂商的单位营收在涨，但其中更大一块要付给代工厂。From HBM4 the base die moves to foundry logic, and custom HBM4E puts the memory controller inside it; TSMC base dies reportedly cost 3–4× SK hynix's core dies, and SK hynix is reportedly evaluating Intel as a second source (it says some reports are untrue)S172S173S183S424B. Memory makers' revenue per unit rises, but a bigger slice goes to the foundry.
  8. 中国变量。The China variable. 长鑫：DRAM 份额约 7–9%，产能约 30–35 万片/月，4Q28 前计划到 50–60 万片；2026 年 bit 增速 48%（三星 24%、SK 海力士 30%、美光 27%）；7 月上市募资 579 亿元、首日 +466%；HBM3E 风险试产、为小米量产 LPDDR6S256S338S249S248S383B。长江存储：2Q26 NAND 出货份额约 14%、首次进入前三，4Q28 前产能约翻倍，计划 2027 上半年上市S273S338S339B。管制：2024-12 HBM 出口规则、三星与 SK 海力士中国工厂的"经验证最终用户"资格 2026 年起取消，长鑫截至年中仍不在实体清单S196S197S351BAC；华为用自研 HBM，灰市 HBM 价格是国际价的数倍，国产 AI 芯片涨价 20–50%S181B。中国 AI 云的需求可能在 2027 年前吸收大部分新增国产产能S419B，但 2028 年后它将是全球价格的主要变量。CXMT: ~7–9% of DRAM, ~300–350K wafers/month heading to 500–600K by 4Q28; 2026 bit growth of 48% (Samsung 24%, SK hynix 30%, Micron 27%); a July listing raised ¥57.9bn and rose 466% on day one; HBM3E in risk production and LPDDR6 in volume for XiaomiS256S338S249S248S383B. YMTC: ~14% of 2Q26 NAND shipments, top three for the first time, roughly doubling capacity by 4Q28, targeting a 1H27 listingS273S338S339B. Controls: the Dec 2024 HBM export rule; Samsung's and SK hynix's China fabs lost validated-end-user status from 2026; CXMT was still not on the Entity List at mid-yearS196S197S351BAC. Huawei uses in-house HBM; grey-market HBM costs several times the international price, and Chinese AI chip prices rose 20–50%S181B. China's AI cloud may absorb most new domestic capacity through 2027S419B, but after 2028 China becomes a major variable for global prices.
  9. 韩国集中度与美国关税。Korea concentration and US tariffs. 三星与 SK 海力士合计约占 DRAM 营收 64%、HBM 83%、NAND 约 47%S207S188S266BD。232 条款自 2026-01-16 起对部分先进芯片征收 25% 关税，第二阶段尚未落地；美国商务部长称存储厂可以"付 100% 关税，或者在美国建厂"S347S348S349B。三星在美投资约 370 亿美元（多为代工）、SK 海力士约 39 亿美元（封装），与台积电 2,650 亿美元相差约 6.5 倍S350B。美光的美国扩产因此是天然的对冲。Samsung and SK hynix hold ~64% of DRAM revenue, 83% of HBM and ~47% of NANDS207S188S266BD. Section 232 has imposed 25% on certain advanced chips since Jan 16, 2026, with a broader phase pending; the US Commerce Secretary said memory makers can "pay a 100 percent tariff, or… build in America"S347S348S349B. Samsung has ~$37bn invested in the US (mostly foundry) and SK hynix ~$3.9bn (packaging), ~6.5× short of TSMC's $265bnS350B. Micron's US build-out is the natural hedge.
  10. 电力与数据中心延期。Power and data-center delays. TrendForce：数据中心电力需求 2026 年 161 GW、2030 年 490.7 GW，AI 服务器占比 2027 年超过 40%，2030 年电网缺口 268 GWS340B。Currence 跟踪 777 个项目：2026 年计划投运约 16 GW，其中 30–50% 可能推迟S148B。存储自身也耗电：厂商称存储最多占计算平台功耗 40%（厂商口径）S341C；SOCAMM2 比 RDIMM 省电超过三分之二S342A。在限电的园区里，每瓦的存储性能直接决定每兆瓦的 token 数。TrendForce: data-center power demand of 161 GW in 2026 and 490.7 GW in 2030, AI servers above 40% of it by 2027, and a 268 GW grid shortfall by 2030S340B. Currence tracks 777 projects: of ~16 GW slated for 2026, 30–50% may slipS148B. Memory itself draws power: a vendor says memory can be up to 40% of a compute platform's power (vendor claim)S341C; SOCAMM2 uses over two-thirds less power than RDIMMS342A. On power-capped sites, memory performance per watt converts directly into tokens per megawatt.
  11. AI 资本开支的融资。Financing AI capex. 过去 12 个月云厂商 32% 的资本开支靠举债（FY24 为 9%），除谷歌与微软外多数自由现金流接近零或为负S331B。TrendForce 的"存储占 CSP 资本开支 2027 年 68%"要求资本开支再增 50%；按其数字，68% × 1.3 万亿美元 ≈ 8,840 亿美元，约为它自己 2027 年存储市场预测的 69%——几乎不给手机、PC 与汽车留空间，口径偏激进S211S329D。Debt funded 32% of hyperscaler capex over the last twelve months (9% in FY24), and most have near-zero or negative free cash flow apart from Alphabet and MicrosoftS331B. TrendForce's "memory at 68% of CSP capex in 2027" needs capex to grow another 50%; on its own numbers, 68% × $1.3tn ≈ $884bn, ~69% of its 2027 memory market forecast, leaving almost nothing for phones, PCs and autos, so the basis looks aggressiveS211S329D.
  12. 2028–2030 年的产能浪潮。The 2028–2030 capacity wave. 五个来源指向 2H27–2028 正常化：Gartner（2027 年底缓解）、IDC（2028 年反弹）、日经（不早于 2028）、TrendForce（2028 年大量产出）、美光（2028 年底起洁净室）S328S265S364S212S161B。Citrini 估三星与 SK 海力士 DRAM 产能到 2030 年约翻倍，叠加中国 2028 年前约翻倍S250S338B。一位基金经理的长期判断是存储是"相当糟糕的行业"S352C——周期没有消失，只是被拉长。Five sources point to normalization in 2H27–2028: Gartner (relief late 2027), IDC (rebound in 2028), Nikkei (no earlier than 2028), TrendForce (substantial output in 2028) and Micron (cleanrooms from late 2028)S328S265S364S212S161B. Citrini sees Samsung and SK hynix DRAM capacity roughly doubling by 2030, on top of China roughly doubling by 2028S250S338B. One fund manager's long-run verdict is that memory is "a pretty dreadful industry"S352C: the cycle has not vanished, only stretched.
  13. 可靠性是一种隐性需求。Reliability is a hidden source of demand. HBM3 是 Llama 3 非计划中断的第二大原因（17.2%）S1A；Gemini 预计静默数据损坏"每一两周一次"S8A。更高的堆叠、更热的封装会让可靠性更难；为容错准备的内存副本、热备节点与本地 NVMe 都是额外的存储需求，却很少出现在需求模型里。HBM3 was the second-largest cause of Llama 3's unplanned interruptions (17.2%)S1A; Gemini expected silent data corruption "every week or two"S8A. Taller stacks and hotter packages make reliability harder; in-memory replicas, hot spares and local NVMe for fault tolerance are extra memory demand that rarely appears in demand models.
  14. 情绪、政策与人。Sentiment, policy and people. 2026-09-12 Anthropic CEO 发文呼吁"为前沿定速"（Pace the Frontier），OpenAI 与 xAI 负责人表示认同S147B；9-14 美光、闪迪各跌约 6%、SK 海力士 ADR 跌约 7%，尽管没有任何云厂商宣布削减资本开支S146B。10-06 SK 海力士再跌约 4–6%，原因包括财报前谨慎、韩元走强与员工奖金计提S149S150B；三星芯片部门在 2Q26 计提了全年营业利润的 10.5% 作为奖金S425B。叠加 DeepSeek（9-10）与东芝（10-02），过去一个月存储股的每一次大跌都来自"预期"而非订单。On Sep 12, 2026 Anthropic's CEO published "We Must Pace the Frontier", and OpenAI's and xAI's leaders voiced agreementS147B; on Sep 14 Micron and SanDisk fell ~6% and SK hynix's ADR ~7%, though no hyperscaler announced capex cutsS146B. On Oct 6 SK hynix fell another ~4–6% on pre-earnings caution, a stronger won and worker-bonus provisionsS149S150B; Samsung's chip division accrued 10.5% of annual operating profit for bonuses in 2Q26S425B. Add DeepSeek (Sep 10) and Toshiba (Oct 2): every big memory selloff in the past month came from expectations, not orders.

产业经济Economics

17

情景推演：2027–2028 年的三条路径Scenarios: three paths for 2027–2028

把前面的变量组合成三条路径。权重是本报告的主观判断，用于表达不确定性的形状，不是预测，也不构成投资建议。The variables above combine into three paths. The weights are our subjective judgment, meant to show the shape of uncertainty; they are not forecasts or investment advice.D

▲让我们上调判断的证据Evidence that would move us up

  1. 2027 年 HBM 长约价格在 +100% 以上全面落地S166。2027 HBM LTAs settle at +100% or more across the boardS166.
  2. NVIDIA 恢复 Vera 1.5 TB 满配或 Rubin Ultra 维持大容量S27S377。NVIDIA restores Vera's full 1.5 TB or keeps Rubin Ultra's large capacityS27S377.
  3. 主流闭源模型的上下文与 Agent 会话时长继续拉长。Leading closed models keep lengthening contexts and agent sessions.

▼让我们下调判断的证据Evidence that would move us down

  1. 合约价环比转为个位数并伴随现货持续低于合约S209S254。Contract growth drops to single digits with spot persistently below contractS209S254.
  2. 两家以上主流模型厂公开采用 V4.1 级别的 KV 压缩S95。Two or more major model makers adopt V4.1-class KV compressionS95.
  3. CSP 下修 2027 年资本开支或融资成本显著上升S331。CSPs cut 2027 capex or financing costs rise sharplyS331.

客观视角 · 非投资建议Objective view · not investment advice

18

市场在定价什么：盈利创纪录，估值按"周期顶"给What markets are pricing: record earnings, valued as a cycle peak

按你的要求，本节只描述市场状态与分歧，不讨论个人配置。核心事实是：存储公司的盈利处在历史最高，远期市盈率却只有 4–8 倍——市场在给"利润能持续多久"打折，而不是怀疑当下的盈利。As you asked, this section only describes market state and disagreements, not personal allocation. The core fact: memory companies' earnings are at all-time highs, yet forward P/Es are only 4–8×. The market is discounting how long profits last, not doubting current earnings.

波动本身是信息Volatility is information

美光 6 月 25 日至 8 月 3 日回撤 32%，三星同期约 −33%S352C；SK 海力士 7 月 10 日在纳斯达克上市（募资约 265 亿美元）后，7 月 13 日首尔股价创纪录单日跌 15%S159S427AB。高盛 9 月 9 日称"最坏的时候可能已经过去"S353B，JPMorgan 认为上行周期"可能持续 5 年以上"S416B。在 4–8 倍远期市盈率下，市场争论的是久期与二阶导，而不是今年的利润。Micron fell 32% from Jun 25 to Aug 3 and Samsung ~33%S352C; after SK hynix listed on Nasdaq on Jul 10 (raising ~$26.5bn), its Seoul shares fell a record 15% in one day on Jul 13S159S427AB. Goldman said on Sep 9 that "the worst of the sector's downturn may be over"S353B, and JPMorgan thinks the upcycle "could last more than five years"S416B. At 4–8× forward earnings, the debate is about duration and the second derivative, not this year's profit.

多空的核心分歧The core disagreement

多方：AI（尤其 Agent）让存储从"周期品"变成"基础设施"，长约与预付款改变了行业结构，2027 年供给最紧。空方：价格斜率已在下降，买家在减配，2028–30 年产能与中国产能集中释放，KV 压缩可能突然改写需求曲线。双方都有一手证据支持，这正是本报告给出跟踪清单（§19）而不是结论性判断的原因。Bulls: AI, especially agents, turns memory from a cyclical commodity into infrastructure; LTAs and prepayments change the industry's structure; 2027 is the tightest year. Bears: the price slope is already falling, buyers are trimming, the 2028–30 and Chinese capacity waves are coming, and KV compression could rewrite the demand curve suddenly. Both sides have primary evidence, which is why this report ends with a tracking list (§19) rather than a verdict.

20

结论Bottom line

AI 把存储从"按 bit 卖的商品"变成了决定算力利用率的约束条件，但它没有消灭周期，只是把周期拉长、把波峰抬高、把利润重新分配。AI has turned memory from a commodity sold by the bit into the constraint that decides how much compute gets used. It has not abolished the cycle; it has stretched it, raised its peaks and redistributed its profits.

  1. 分层（Q1a）。The tiers (Q1a). 九层存储各有物理极限与产业结构。最难造、最缺的是 HBM（八道工艺关、每 bit 约 2.5–3 倍晶圆、三家供应）；其次是被 HBM 挤压又被 Agent 重新需要的 CPU 侧 DRAM；再其次是因 KV 缓存而"上移"的企业级 NAND 与被数据留存拉动的近线 HDD。SRAM 受先进逻辑约束、CXL 是效率层、新型介质 2028 年前无法替代主流。Each of the nine tiers has its own physical limits and industry structure. HBM is the hardest to make and the scarcest (eight process gates, ~2.5–3× wafer per bit, three suppliers); next is CPU-side DRAM, squeezed by HBM and needed again by agents; then enterprise NAND, pushed up a tier by the KV cache, and nearline HDD, pulled by data retention. SRAM is bound by leading-edge logic, CXL is an efficiency layer, and new media cannot replace the mainstream before 2028.
  2. 流程与瓶颈（Q1b/Q1c）。Workflows and bottlenecks (Q1b/Q1c). 训练卡在 HBM 容量、互联与容错；推理的 decode 卡在 HBM 带宽，多轮与长上下文卡在 KV 容量；Agent 把 KV 的保留时间从秒拉长到小时，把需求扩散到 CPU 内存与闪存。21 个阶段里 HBM 在 6 个阶段是"关键"，DRAM 在 8 个阶段承压，NAND 的压力集中在"保留"，HDD 的压力来自"留存"。Training is bound by HBM capacity, interconnect and fault tolerance; inference decode by HBM bandwidth, and multi-turn and long-context work by KV capacity; agents stretch KV retention from seconds to hours and spread demand into CPU memory and flash. Across 21 stages HBM is critical in six, DRAM is pressured in eight, NAND's pressure concentrates in retention and HDD's in data keeping.
  3. 方向。Directions. HBM：更快（HBM4E）、更高（16–20 层）、更"逻辑化"（定制基底、近存计算）；DRAM：32Gb 颗粒、LPDDR6/SOCAMM2、MRDIMM、4F² 与 3D；NAND：500–1,000 层、PCIe 6/7、1 亿 IOPS、PB 级 QLC、HBF；HDD：HAMR 走向 100 TB；SRAM：堆叠与新材料。HBM: faster (HBM4E), taller (16–20 layers), more logic-like (custom base dies, near-memory compute). DRAM: 32Gb dies, LPDDR6/SOCAMM2, MRDIMM, 4F² and 3D. NAND: 500–1,000 layers, PCIe 6/7, 100M IOPS, PB-class QLC, HBF. HDD: HAMR towards 100 TB. SRAM: stacking and new materials.
  4. 绕行。Workarounds. 算法压缩每 token 存储的速度很快（DeepSeek 约 8–12 倍/年），但需求增长（谷歌 token 约 16 倍/年、上下文 128K→1M）至今更快。多数架构路径是在层间"搬需求"。最大的不确定性在 KV 闪存：它对模型结构的敏感度高达一到两个数量级。Algorithms shrink memory per token fast (DeepSeek ~8–12× a year), but demand growth (Google tokens ~16× a year, contexts 128K→1M) has been faster so far. Most architectural paths move demand between tiers. The biggest uncertainty is KV flash, whose sensitivity to model architecture spans one to two orders of magnitude.
  5. 容易忽略的。Easy to miss. 涨价斜率在急降、长约既托底也封顶、买家在设计掉存储、消费电子被摧毁、中国产能 2028 年后成为全球变量、2028–30 年产能浪潮、AI 资本开支的融资与电力约束。它们决定周期的长度与利润的分配，比"缺不缺"更能解释股价。Falling price slopes, LTAs that set both floors and ceilings, buyers designing memory out, destroyed consumer demand, Chinese capacity becoming a global variable after 2028, the 2028–30 capacity wave, and financing and power limits on AI capex. They decide the cycle's length and the split of profits, and explain stock prices better than whether memory is short.
  6. 如何使用本报告。How to use this report. 用 §19 的清单逐条核对；用 M1–M6 自己改变量检验结论；供需数字与你的《缺口的算术》v4.2 交叉核对时，以你的 Excel 为准。Check §19 line by line; use M1–M6 to test conclusions with your own inputs; when cross-checking supply-demand numbers against your Gap Arithmetic v4.2, your Excel prevails.
21

附录：术语、公式、口径冲突与资料来源Appendix: glossary, formulas, conflicts and sources

A. 术语表A. Glossary

术语Term含义Meaning
KV 缓存KV cache推理时缓存的历史 token 的 Key/Value 向量，避免重复计算；随上下文长度与并发线性增长Cached Key/Value vectors of past tokens during inference, avoiding recomputation; grows linearly with context and concurrency
Prefill / Decode一次性处理输入（算力受限）/ 逐 token 生成（带宽受限）Processing the input at once (compute-bound) / generating token by token (bandwidth-bound)
TTFT / TPOT首 token 延迟 / 每个输出 token 的时间Time to first token / time per output token
MoE混合专家：每个 token 只激活部分参数，总参数决定容量需求、激活参数决定带宽需求Mixture of experts: each token activates only some parameters; total parameters set capacity, active ones set bandwidth
MHA / GQA / MLA多头 / 分组查询 / 多头潜在注意力；KV 依次变小（MLA 比同结构 MHA 小约 71 倍）Multi-head / grouped-query / multi-head latent attention; KV shrinks in that order (MLA ~71× smaller than equivalent MHA)
ZeRO / FSDP把模型状态切分到数据并行的各卡，用通信换显存Shards model state across data-parallel devices, trading communication for memory
TSV硅通孔，垂直穿过裸片的导电通道，HBM 堆叠的基础Through-silicon via, a vertical conductor through a die; the basis of HBM stacking
MR-MUF / TC-NCF / 混合键合hybrid bonding回流焊+模塑填充 / 热压+非导电膜 / 铜对铜直接键合（间距 <1 µm）Reflow plus molded underfill / thermo-compression with non-conductive film / direct copper-to-copper bonding (<1 µm pitch)
基底裸片Base dieHBM 堆栈最底层的逻辑裸片，负责接口与控制；HBM4 起转向代工逻辑工艺The logic die at the bottom of an HBM stack handling interface and control; moves to foundry logic from HBM4
CoWoS台积电的硅中介层先进封装，把 GPU 与 HBM 放在同一封装内TSMC's silicon-interposer packaging that puts GPU and HBM in one package
EUV / High-NA极紫外光刻 / 高数值孔径 EUV（DRAM 约 2028 年导入量产）Extreme-ultraviolet lithography / high-numerical-aperture EUV (into DRAM volume ~2028)
4F² / VCT单元面积 4F² 的垂直沟道晶体管 DRAM，比 6F² 密度高 30–50%Vertical-channel-transistor DRAM with a 4F² cell, 30–50% denser than 6F²
CBA / 晶圆键合wafer bondingNAND 的 CMOS 与存储阵列分别在两片晶圆上制造再键合NAND's CMOS and array built on separate wafers and then bonded
TLC / QLC / PLC每单元 3 / 4 / 5 bit；bit 越多越便宜，写入越慢、寿命越短3 / 4 / 5 bits per cell; more bits are cheaper but slower to write and less durable
DWPD每日全盘写入次数，衡量 SSD 写入寿命Drive writes per day, a measure of SSD endurance
HAMR / SMR热辅助磁记录 / 叠瓦式记录（同碟片多 10–20% 容量）Heat-assisted magnetic recording / shingled recording (10–20% more capacity per platter)
CXL基于 PCIe 物理层的缓存一致性互联，用于内存扩展与池化A cache-coherent interconnect on the PCIe PHY for memory expansion and pooling
SOCAMM / MRDIMM可拆卸 LPDDR 服务器模组 / 多路复用双列内存模组Detachable LPDDR server module / multiplexed-rank DIMM
HBF高带宽闪存：NAND 按 HBM 方式堆叠，读多写少场景High Bandwidth Flash: NAND stacked HBM-style, for read-mostly workloads
PIM / CIM存内处理（在内存旁加计算）/ 存内计算（在存储阵列内计算）Processing in memory (compute beside memory) / compute in memory (compute inside the array)
长约 LTA / 照付不议LTA / take-or-pay多年期供货协议；买方须按约定量付款，常含预付款与价格区间Multi-year supply agreements; buyers pay for committed volume, often with prepayments and price bands
充足率Sufficiency ratio（供给 − 需求）÷ 需求；负值表示短缺(Supply − demand) ÷ demand; negative means shortage
wpm / EB / Gb vs GB片/月 / 艾字节（10¹⁸ 字节）/ 1 GB = 8 GbWafers per month / exabyte (10¹⁸ bytes) / 1 GB = 8 Gb

每 token KVKV per token2 × L × Hkv × dhead × bytes（MLA：L × (dc + dR) × bytes） (MLA: L × (dc + dR) × bytes)
decode 步频上限Decode step-rate ceilingBWHBM × η ÷ (Wread + Σ KV)
模型状态Model stateΨ × (w + g + o)，混合精度 Adam 为 2 + 2 + 12 = 16 字节; mixed-precision Adam: 2 + 2 + 12 = 16 bytes

D. 方法与边界D. Method and limits

  • 数据截至 2026-10-07（北京时间）。三星 3Q26 初步业绩（10-08 韩国时间）与 OCP 全球峰会 2026 未纳入。Data as of Oct 7, 2026 (Beijing time). Samsung's 3Q26 preliminary results (Oct 8 KST) and OCP Global Summit 2026 are not included.
  • 优先采用公司、标准组织、监管与论文等一手来源；二手来源均标注等级；存在冲突的数字并列展示并在上表登记。Primary sources (companies, standards bodies, regulators, papers) come first; secondary sources are graded; conflicting numbers are shown side by side and logged above.
  • 标注 D 的数字为本报告推算，公式已注明；六个交互模型用于理解量级与敏感度，不是预测。Numbers marked D are our calculations with formulas shown; the six interactive models are for orders of magnitude and sensitivity, not forecasts.
  • 与你的《缺口的算术》（最新 v4.2，2026-10-01，按美光 FQ4 FY26 指引重标）口径不同；凡供需数字冲突，以你的 Excel 为准。Bases differ from your Gap Arithmetic model (latest v4.2, Oct 1, 2026, recalibrated to Micron's FQ4 FY26 guidance); where supply-demand numbers conflict, your Excel prevails.
  • 本报告仅供研究，不构成任何投资建议，也不考虑任何个人的资产配置。This report is for research only, is not investment advice and does not consider anyone's personal allocation.
